40	function formatCount(n: number): string {
41	  if (n < 1000) return String(n);

43	}

45	export function ExtHero({ ext, locale = "en" }: ExtHeroProps) {

51	    : false;

54	
55	  return (
56	    <header className="bg-card border-border relative overflow-hidden rounded-xl border p-7 shadow-[0_1px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.02)]">

60	      />

63	          className="flex size-16 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border-[1.5px] text-2xl"
64	          style={{
65	            background: `${ext.iconColor ?? "#888"}1c`,
66	            borderColor: `${ext.iconColor ?? "#888"}33`,
67	          }}
68	        >
69	          {ext.iconEmoji}

72	          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">

76	                className={cn(
77	                  "rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold",

79	                )}
80	              >
81	                {BADGE_LABEL[ext.badge]}
82	              </span>
83	            )}
84	            <span className="bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold">
85	              {CATEGORY_LABEL[ext.category]}
86	            </span>
87	          </div>
88	          {(ext.tagline || desc) && (
89	            <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-2 max-w-3xl text-[14px] leading-relaxed">
90	              {ext.tagline ?? desc}
91	            </p>
92	          )}
93	
94	          <div className="text-muted-foreground mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-5 gap-y-2 text-[12.5px]">
95	            <span>

113	                    : "border-border bg-secondary",
114	                )}
115	              >
116	                <Building2 className="size-3" />
117	                <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold">{deptLeaf}</span>

3	import { ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";
4	import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
5	
6	import { deptPath } from "@/lib/data/departments";

8	import type { ExtensionDetail } from "@/lib/db/queries/extensions";

10	import type { Locale } from "@/types";
11	
12	interface ExtMetadataPanelProps {
13	  ext: ExtensionDetail;
14	  locale?: Locale;
15	}
16	
17	export function ExtMetadataPanel({
18	  ext,
19	  locale = "en",
20	}: ExtMetadataPanelProps) {
21	  const t = useTranslations("metadata");
22	  const tx = useTranslations("taxonomy");
23	  const deptTrail = ext.deptId ? deptPath(ext.deptId, locale) : null;
24	
25	  return (
26	    <aside className="text-[12.5px]">
27	      <Section title={t("sectionTitle")}>
28	        <Row label={t("slug")}>
29	          <code className="bg-muted rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11.5px]">
30	            {ext.slug}
31	          </code>
32	        </Row>
33	        <Row label={t("license")}>
34	          {ext.licenseSpdx ?? <Empty />}
35	        </Row>
36	        <Row label={t("homepage")}>
37	          {ext.homepageUrl ? <ExtLink href={ext.homepageUrl} /> : <Empty />}
38	        </Row>
39	        <Row label={t("repository")}>
40	          {ext.repoUrl ? <ExtLink href={ext.repoUrl} /> : <Empty />}
41	        </Row>
42	        <Row label={t("compatibility")}>
43	          {ext.compatibilityJson ? (
44	            <code className="bg-muted block rounded p-2 font-mono text-[11px] whitespace-pre-wrap">
45	              {JSON.stringify(ext.compatibilityJson, null, 2)}
46	            </code>
47	          ) : (
48	            <Empty />
49	          )}
50	        </Row>
51	      </Section>
52	
53	      <Section title={t("categorizationTitle")}>
54	        <Row label={t("scope")}>{t(`scopeValues.${ext.scope}`)}</Row>
55	        <Row label={t("functional")}>
56	          <span className="text-foreground font-semibold">
57	            {tx(`funcCat.${ext.funcCat}`)}
58	          </span>
59	          <span className="text-muted-foreground"> / {tx(`l1.${ext.subCat}`)}</span>
60	          {ext.l2 && (
61	            <span className="text-muted-foreground"> / {tx(`l2.${ext.l2}`)}</span>
62	          )}
63	        </Row>
64	        {deptTrail && (
65	          <Row label={t("department")}>
66	            <span className="text-muted-foreground">
67	              {deptTrail.slice(0, -1).join(" / ")}
68	              {deptTrail.length > 1 ? " / " : ""}
69	            </span>
70	            <span className="text-foreground font-semibold">
71	              {deptTrail[deptTrail.length - 1]}
72	            </span>
73	          </Row>
74	        )}
75	      </Section>
76	
77	      {ext.tagIds.length > 0 && (
78	        <Section title={t("tagsTitle")}>
79	          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
80	            {ext.tagIds.map((tag) => (
81	              <Link
82	                key={tag}
83	                href={`/extensions?tags=${encodeURIComponent(tag)}&dept=__all`}
84	                className="border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary hover:text-primary rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold transition"
85	              >
86	                #{tagLabel(tag, locale)}
87	              </Link>
88	            ))}
89	          </div>
90	        </Section>
91	      )}
92	    </aside>
93	  );
94	}
95	
96	function Section({
97	  title,
98	  children,
99	}: {
100	  title: string;
101	  children: React.ReactNode;
102	}) {
103	  return (
104	    <div className="border-border mb-5 border-b pb-5 last:mb-0 last:border-b-0 last:pb-0">
105	      <h3 className="text-muted-foreground mb-3 text-[10.5px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase">
106	        {title}
107	      </h3>
108	      <dl className="flex flex-col gap-2.5">{children}</dl>
109	    </div>
110	  );
111	}
112	
113	function Row({
114	  label,
115	  children,
116	}: {
117	  label: string;
118	  children: React.ReactNode;
119	}) {
120	  return (
121	    <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
122	      <dt className="text-muted-foreground text-[10.5px] font-semibold tracking-wide uppercase">
123	        {label}
124	      </dt>
125	      <dd className="text-foreground/85 text-[12.5px]">{children}</dd>
126	    </div>
127	  );
128	}
129	
130	function Empty() {
131	  return <span className="text-muted-foreground/60">—</span>;
132	}
133	
134	function ExtLink({ href }: { href: string }) {
135	  return (
136	    <a
137	      href={href}
138	      target="_blank"
139	      rel="noopener noreferrer"
140	      className="text-primary inline-flex items-center gap-1 break-all hover:underline"
141	    >
142	      <span className="truncate">{href.replace(/^https?:\/\//, "")}</span>
143	      <ExternalLink className="size-3 shrink-0" />
144	    </a>
145	  );
146	}

10	
11	export default async function ExtensionDetailPage({
12	  params,
13	}: {
14	  params: Promise<{ locale: string; slug: string }>;
15	}) {
16	  const { locale, slug } = await params;
17	  const [ext, t] = await Promise.all([
18	    getExtensionBySlug(slug),
19	    getTranslations("extensions"),
20	  ]);
21	  if (!ext) notFound();
22	
23	  return (

26	        <Link href="/extensions" className="hover:text-foreground transition">
27	          {t("breadcrumb")}
28	        </Link>
29	        <span className="opacity-50">/</span>
30	        <span className="text-foreground">
31	          {locale === "zh" && ext.nameZh ? ext.nameZh : ext.name}
32	        </span>
33	      </nav>
34	
35	      <ExtHero ext={ext} locale={locale as Locale} />
36	
37	      <div className="mt-7 grid gap-10 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_280px]">
38	        <main className="min-w-0">
39	          {ext.readmeMd ? (
40	            <MarkdownContent>{ext.readmeMd}</MarkdownContent>
41	          ) : (
42	            <p className="text-muted-foreground py-12 text-center text-[13px]">
43	              {t("noReadme")}
44	            </p>
45	          )}
46	
47	          <section className="border-border mt-10 border-t pt-6">
48	            <h2 className="serif mb-3 text-xl tracking-tight">
49	              {t("versionsTitle")}
50	            </h2>
51	            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-[13px]">
52	              {t("noVersions")}
53	            </p>
54	          </section>
55	        </main>
56	
57	        <ExtMetadataPanel ext={ext} locale={locale as Locale} />
58	      </div>
59	    </div>
60	  );
61	}
62	
63	export async function generateMetadata({
64	  params,
65	}: {
66	  params: Promise<{ locale: string; slug: string }>;
67	}) {
68	  const { locale, slug } = await params;
69	  const ext = await getExtensionBySlug(slug);
70	  if (!ext) return { title: "Not found · AgentCenter" };
71	  const name = locale === "zh" && ext.nameZh ? ext.nameZh : ext.name;
72	  return {
73	    title: `${name} · AgentCenter`,
74	    description: ext.tagline ?? ext.description ?? undefined,
75	  };
76	}

1	"use client";
2	
3	import { Check, Download } from "lucide-react";
4	import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
5	import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
6	
7	import { installExtension } from "@/lib/actions/install";
8	import { useRouter } from "@/lib/i18n/navigation";
9	
10	type Size = "sm" | "lg";
11	
12	interface InstallButtonProps {
13	  extensionId: string;
14	  size?: Size;
15	}
16	
17	export function InstallButton({
18	  extensionId,
19	  size = "sm",
20	}: InstallButtonProps) {
21	  const t = useTranslations("common");
22	  const router = useRouter();
23	  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();
24	  const [installed, setInstalled] = useState(false);
25	
26	  async function handleClick() {
27	    startTransition(async () => {
28	      const result = await installExtension(extensionId);
29	
30	      if (!result.ok && result.error === "unauthenticated") {
31	        router.push("/sign-in");
32	        return;
33	      }
34	
35	      if (result.ok) {
36	        setInstalled(true);
37	        // Reset after 3 s so the user can see the confirmation.
38	        setTimeout(() => setInstalled(false), 3000);
39	      }
40	    });
41	  }
42	
43	  if (size === "lg") {
44	    return (
45	      <button
46	        type="button"
47	        onClick={handleClick}
48	        disabled={isPending || installed}
49	        className="bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:opacity-90 inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-bold transition disabled:opacity-70"
50	      >
51	        {installed ? (
52	          <Check className="size-3.5" />
53	        ) : (
54	          <Download className="size-3.5" />
55	        )}
56	        {isPending
57	          ? t("installing")
58	          : installed
59	            ? t("installed")
60	            : t("install")}
61	      </button>
62	    );
63	  }
64	
65	  return (
66	    <button
67	      type="button"
68	      onClick={handleClick}
69	      disabled={isPending || installed}
70	      className="bg-primary text-primary-foreground flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-bold transition hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-70"
71	    >
72	      {installed ? (
73	        <Check className="size-3" />
74	      ) : (
75	        <Download className="size-3" />
76	      )}
77	      {isPending
78	        ? t("installing")
79	        : installed
80	          ? t("installed")
81	          : t("install")}
82	    </button>
83	  );
84	}
85	
```

1	import { count, desc, eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
2	
3	import { db } from "@/lib/db/client";
4	import { extensions, extensionTags } from "@/lib/db/schema";
5	import {
6	  buildExtensionOrder,
7	  buildExtensionWhere,
8	} from "@/lib/search/query";
9	import {
10	  PAGE_SIZE,
11	  pageOffset,
12	  type Filters,
13	} from "@/lib/validators/filters";
14	
15	const listSelect = {
16	  id: extensions.id,
17	  slug: extensions.slug,
18	  category: extensions.category,
19	  badge: extensions.badge,
20	  scope: extensions.scope,
21	  funcCat: extensions.funcCat,
22	  subCat: extensions.subCat,
23	  l2: extensions.l2,
24	  deptId: extensions.deptId,
25	  iconEmoji: extensions.iconEmoji,
26	  iconColor: extensions.iconColor,
27	  name: extensions.name,
28	  nameZh: extensions.nameZh,
29	  description: extensions.description,
30	  descriptionZh: extensions.descriptionZh,
31	  downloadsCount: extensions.downloadsCount,
32	  starsAvg: extensions.starsAvg,
33	  // array_agg returns null when the LEFT JOIN finds no tags; coalesce to '{}'.
34	  tagIds: sql<string[]>`coalesce(array_agg(${extensionTags.tagId}) FILTER (WHERE ${extensionTags.tagId} IS NOT NULL), '{}')`,
35	};
36	
37	export async function listExtensions(
38	  filters: Filters,
39	  userDeptId?: string,
40	) {
41	  const where = buildExtensionWhere(filters, userDeptId);
42	  const order = buildExtensionOrder(filters.sort);
43	
44	  return db
45	    .select(listSelect)
46	    .from(extensions)
47	    .leftJoin(
48	      extensionTags,
49	      eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id),
50	    )
51	    .where(where)
52	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
53	    .orderBy(...order)
54	    .limit(PAGE_SIZE)
55	    .offset(pageOffset(filters.page));
56	}
57	
58	export type ExtensionListItem = Awaited<
59	  ReturnType<typeof listExtensions>
60	>[number];
61	
62	export async function countFilteredExtensions(
63	  filters: Filters,
64	  userDeptId?: string,
65	) {
66	  const where = buildExtensionWhere(filters, userDeptId);
67	  const [row] = await db
68	    .select({ count: count() })
69	    .from(extensions)
70	    .where(where);
71	  return Number(row?.count ?? 0);
72	}
73	
74	export async function getFeaturedExtension() {
75	  const [row] = await db
76	    .select({
77	      id: extensions.id,
78	      slug: extensions.slug,
79	      name: extensions.name,
80	      nameZh: extensions.nameZh,
81	      description: extensions.description,
82	      descriptionZh: extensions.descriptionZh,
83	      iconEmoji: extensions.iconEmoji,
84	      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,
85	      downloadsCount: extensions.downloadsCount,
86	    })
87	    .from(extensions)
88	    .where(eq(extensions.badge, "official"))
89	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.downloadsCount))
90	    .limit(1);
91	  return row ?? null;
92	}
93	
94	export async function getExtensionBySlug(slug: string) {
95	  const [row] = await db
96	    .select({
97	      id: extensions.id,
98	      slug: extensions.slug,
99	      category: extensions.category,
100	      badge: extensions.badge,
101	      scope: extensions.scope,
102	      funcCat: extensions.funcCat,
103	      subCat: extensions.subCat,
104	      l2: extensions.l2,
105	      deptId: extensions.deptId,
106	      iconEmoji: extensions.iconEmoji,
107	      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,
108	      name: extensions.name,
109	      nameZh: extensions.nameZh,
110	      tagline: extensions.tagline,
111	      taglineZh: extensions.taglineZh,
112	      description: extensions.description,
113	      descriptionZh: extensions.descriptionZh,
114	      readmeMd: extensions.readmeMd,
115	      homepageUrl: extensions.homepageUrl,
116	      repoUrl: extensions.repoUrl,
117	      licenseSpdx: extensions.licenseSpdx,
118	      compatibilityJson: extensions.compatibilityJson,
119	      downloadsCount: extensions.downloadsCount,
120	      starsAvg: extensions.starsAvg,
121	      ratingsCount: extensions.ratingsCount,
122	      publishedAt: extensions.publishedAt,
123	      tagIds: sql<string[]>`coalesce(array_agg(${extensionTags.tagId}) FILTER (WHERE ${extensionTags.tagId} IS NOT NULL), '{}')`,
124	    })
125	    .from(extensions)
126	    .leftJoin(
127	      extensionTags,
128	      eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id),
129	    )
130	    .where(eq(extensions.slug, slug))
131	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
132	    .limit(1);
133	  return row ?? null;
134	}
135	
136	export type ExtensionDetail = NonNullable<
137	  Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getExtensionBySlug>>
138	>;
139	
140	export async function countPublishedExtensions() {
141	  const [row] = await db
142	    .select({ count: count() })
143	    .from(extensions)
144	    .where(eq(extensions.visibility, "published"));
145	  return Number(row?.count ?? 0);
146	}
147	
```

1	"use client";
2	
3	import { Folder, FolderCheck } from "lucide-react";
4	import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
5	import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
6	
7	import { saveExtension } from "@/lib/actions/collections";
8	import { useRouter } from "@/lib/i18n/navigation";
9	
10	export function SaveButton({ extensionId }: { extensionId: string }) {
11	  const t = useTranslations("common");
12	  const router = useRouter();
13	  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();
14	  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
15	
16	  async function handleClick() {
17	    startTransition(async () => {
18	      const result = await saveExtension(extensionId);
19	
20	      if (!result.ok && result.error === "unauthenticated") {
21	        router.push("/sign-in");
22	        return;
23	      }
24	
25	      if (result.ok) {
26	        setSaved(true);
27	        setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 3000);
28	      }
29	    });
30	  }
31	
32	  return (
33	    <button
34	      type="button"
35	      onClick={handleClick}
36	      disabled={isPending}
37	      aria-label={saved ? t("saved") : t("addToGroup")}
38	      className="bg-secondary border-border hover:bg-accent flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-md border transition-colors disabled:opacity-60"
39	    >
40	      {saved ? (
41	        <FolderCheck className="text-primary size-3.5" />
42	      ) : (
43	        <Folder className="text-muted-foreground size-3.5" />
44	      )}
45	    </button>
46	  );
47	}
48	
```

1	import { Building2, Download, Star } from "lucide-react";
2	
3	import { Link } from "@/lib/i18n/navigation";
4	
5	import { deptPath, MY_DEPT_ID } from "@/lib/data/departments";
6	import type { ExtensionListItem } from "@/lib/db/queries/extensions";
7	import { tagLabel } from "@/lib/tags";
8	import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
9	import type { Locale } from "@/types";
10	
11	import { InstallButton } from "./install-button";
12	import { SaveButton } from "./save-button";
13	
14	interface ExtCardProps {
15	  ext: ExtensionListItem;
16	  locale?: Locale;
17	}
18	
19	const BADGE_LABEL: Record<NonNullable<ExtensionListItem["badge"]>, string> = {
20	  official: "Official",
21	  popular: "Popular",
22	  new: "New",
23	};
24	
25	const BADGE_CLASS: Record<NonNullable<ExtensionListItem["badge"]>, string> = {
26	  official: "badge-official",
27	  popular: "badge-popular",
28	  new: "badge-new",
29	};
30	
31	function formatCount(n: number): string {
32	  if (n < 1000) return String(n);
33	  return `${(n / 1000).toFixed(n >= 100000 ? 0 : 1)}k`;
34	}
35	
36	export function ExtCard({ ext, locale = "en" }: ExtCardProps) {
37	  const name = locale === "zh" && ext.nameZh ? ext.nameZh : ext.name;
38	  const desc =
39	    locale === "zh" && ext.descriptionZh ? ext.descriptionZh : ext.description;
40	
41	  const isMine = ext.deptId
42	    ? ext.deptId === MY_DEPT_ID || ext.deptId.startsWith(`${MY_DEPT_ID}.`)
43	    : false;
44	  const deptTrail = ext.deptId ? deptPath(ext.deptId, locale) : null;
45	  const deptLeaf = deptTrail?.[deptTrail.length - 1];
46	
47	  return (
48	    <article className="bg-card border-border hover:border-primary/30 flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-[10px] border p-[18px] transition-all duration-200 ease-out hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-[0_12px_28px_-10px_color-mix(in_oklab,var(--primary)_30%,transparent)]">
49	      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
50	        <div
51	          className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-[7px] border-[1.5px] text-[17px]"
52	          style={{
53	            background: `${ext.iconColor ?? "#888"}1c`,
54	            borderColor: `${ext.iconColor ?? "#888"}33`,
55	          }}
56	        >
57	          {ext.iconEmoji}
58	        </div>
59	        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
60	          <div className="mb-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
61	            <Link
62	              href={`/extensions/${ext.slug}`}
63	              className="hover:text-primary truncate text-[14px] font-bold transition-colors"
64	            >
65	              {name}
66	            </Link>
67	            {ext.badge && (
68	              <span
69	                className={cn(
70	                  "shrink-0 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold",
71	                  BADGE_CLASS[ext.badge],
72	                )}
73	              >
74	                {BADGE_LABEL[ext.badge]}
75	              </span>
76	            )}
77	          </div>
78	          {deptLeaf && (
79	            <div
80	              className={cn(
81	                "mt-1 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded border px-2 py-0.5",
82	                isMine
83	                  ? "border-primary/25 bg-primary/10 text-primary"
84	                  : "border-border bg-secondary text-muted-foreground",
85	              )}
86	            >
87	              <Building2 className="size-3" />
88	              <span className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold">{deptLeaf}</span>
89	            </div>
90	          )}
91	        </div>
92	      </div>
93	
94	      {desc && (
95	        <p className="text-muted-foreground line-clamp-2 text-[12.5px] leading-[1.55]">
96	          {desc}
97	        </p>
98	      )}
99	
100	      {ext.tagIds.length > 0 && (
101	        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
102	          {ext.tagIds.map((tag) => (
103	            <span
104	              key={tag}
105	              className="border-border text-muted-foreground rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10.5px] font-semibold"
106	            >
107	              #{tagLabel(tag, locale)}
108	            </span>
109	          ))}
110	        </div>
111	      )}
112	
113	      <div className="mt-auto flex items-center gap-3 pt-1">
114	        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
115	          <Star className="size-3 fill-amber-500 text-amber-500" />
116	          <span className="text-[12px] font-semibold">
117	            {Number(ext.starsAvg).toFixed(1)}
118	          </span>
119	        </div>
120	        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
121	          <Download className="text-muted-foreground size-3" />
122	          <span className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-[12px]">
123	            {formatCount(ext.downloadsCount)}
124	          </span>
125	        </div>
126	        <div className="ml-auto flex gap-1.5">
127	          <SaveButton extensionId={ext.id} />
128	          <InstallButton extensionId={ext.id} size="sm" />
129	        </div>
130	      </div>
131	    </article>
132	  );
133	}
134

1	import Markdown from "react-markdown";
2	import rehypeSanitize from "rehype-sanitize";
3	import remarkGfm from "remark-gfm";
4	
5	interface MarkdownContentProps {
6	  children: string;
7	}
8	
9	export function MarkdownContent({ children }: MarkdownContentProps) {
10	  return (
11	    <div className="text-foreground">
12	      <Markdown
13	        remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}
14	        rehypePlugins={[rehypeSanitize]}
15	        components={{
16	          h1: ({ children }) => (
17	            <h1 className="serif mt-8 mb-3 text-3xl tracking-tight first:mt-0">
18	              {children}
19	            </h1>
20	          ),
21	          h2: ({ children }) => (
22	            <h2 className="serif mt-7 mb-2 text-xl tracking-tight">
23	              {children}
24	            </h2>
25	          ),
26	          h3: ({ children }) => (
27	            <h3 className="mt-5 mb-2 text-base font-semibold">{children}</h3>
28	          ),
29	          p: ({ children }) => (
30	            <p className="text-foreground/85 mb-3 text-[14px] leading-[1.7]">
31	              {children}
32	            </p>
33	          ),
34	          ul: ({ children }) => (
35	            <ul className="text-foreground/85 mb-3 list-disc pl-5 text-[14px] leading-[1.7]">
36	              {children}
37	            </ul>
38	          ),
39	          ol: ({ children }) => (
40	            <ol className="text-foreground/85 mb-3 list-decimal pl-5 text-[14px] leading-[1.7]">
41	              {children}
42	            </ol>
43	          ),
44	          li: ({ children }) => <li className="mb-1">{children}</li>,
45	          code: ({ children, className }) => {
46	            const isBlock = className?.startsWith("language-");
47	            if (isBlock) {
48	              return <code className={className}>{children}</code>;
49	            }
50	            return (
51	              <code className="bg-muted text-foreground rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-[12.5px]">
52	                {children}
53	              </code>
54	            );
55	          },
56	          pre: ({ children }) => (
57	            <pre className="bg-muted border-border my-4 overflow-x-auto rounded-md border p-3 font-mono text-[12.5px] leading-[1.55]">
58	              {children}
59	            </pre>
60	          ),
61	          a: ({ children, href }) => (
62	            <a
63	              href={href}
64	              className="text-primary underline-offset-2 hover:underline"
65	              target={href?.startsWith("http") ? "_blank" : undefined}
66	              rel={href?.startsWith("http") ? "noopener noreferrer" : undefined}
67	            >
68	              {children}
69	            </a>
70	          ),
71	          strong: ({ children }) => (
72	            <strong className="text-foreground font-semibold">{children}</strong>
73	          ),
74	          em: ({ children }) => <em className="italic">{children}</em>,
75	          hr: () => <hr className="border-border my-6" />,
76	          blockquote: ({ children }) => (
77	            <blockquote className="border-primary/40 text-muted-foreground my-4 border-l-2 pl-4 text-[13.5px] italic">
78	              {children}
79	            </blockquote>
80	          ),
81	          table: ({ children }) => (
82	            <div className="my-4 overflow-x-auto">
83	              <table className="border-border w-full border-collapse border text-[13px]">

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/db/queries/extensions.ts",
  "old_string": "export type ExtensionDetail = NonNullable<\n  Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getExtensionBySlug>>\n>;",
  "new_string": "export type ExtensionDetail = NonNullable<\n  Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getExtensionBySlug>>\n>;\n\nexport async function getLatestExtensionVersion(extensionId: string) {\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({\n      version: extensionVersions.version,\n      changelog: extensionVersions.changelog,\n      changelogZh: extensionVersions.changelogZh,\n      publishedAt: extensionVersions.publishedAt,\n      bundleSize: files.size,\n    })\n    .from(extensionVersions)\n    .leftJoin(files, eq(files.id, extensionVersions.bundleFileId))\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensionId),\n        eq(extensionVersions.status, \"ready\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.publishedAt))\n    .limit(1);\n  return row ?? null;\n}\n\nexport async function listExtensionVersions(extensionId: string) {\n  return db\n    .select({\n      version: extensionVersions.version,\n      changelog: extensionVersions.changelog,\n      changelogZh: extensionVersions.changelogZh,\n      publishedAt: extensionVersions.publishedAt,\n    })\n    .from(extensionVersions)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensionId),\n        eq(extensionVersions.status, \"ready\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.publishedAt))\n    .limit(20);\n}\n\nexport type ExtensionVersionRow = Awaited<\n  ReturnType<typeof listExtensionVersions>\n>[number];\n\nexport async function getRelatedExtensions(\n  extensionId: string,\n  category: ExtensionDetail[\"category\"],\n  limit = 4,\n) {\n  return db\n    .select({\n      id: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      nameZh: extensions.nameZh,\n      iconEmoji: extensions.iconEmoji,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      starsAvg: extensions.starsAvg,\n      downloadsCount: extensions.downloadsCount,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(extensions.category, category),\n        eq(extensions.visibility, \"published\"),\n        ne(extensions.id, extensionId),\n      ),\n    )\n    .orderBy(desc(extensions.downloadsCount))\n    .limit(limit);\n}\n\nexport type RelatedExtension = Awaited<\n  ReturnType<typeof getRelatedExtensions>\n>[number];"
}

  "content": "import { Building2, Clock, Download, Star, Tag } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nimport { InstallButton } from \"./install-button\";\nimport { SaveButton } from \"./save-button\";\nimport { ShareButton } from \"./share-button\";\n\nimport { deptPath, MY_DEPT_ID } from \"@/lib/data/departments\";\nimport type { ExtensionDetail } from \"@/lib/db/queries/extensions\";\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\nimport type { Locale } from \"@/types\";\n\ninterface ExtHeroProps {\n  ext: ExtensionDetail;\n  locale?: Locale;\n  latestVersion?: string | null;\n  publishedAt?: Date | null;\n  shareUrl: string;\n  shareLabel: string;\n  copiedLabel: string;\n  verifiedLabel: string;\n  ratingLabel: string;\n  downloadsLabel: string;\n  versionLabel: string;\n  updatedLabel: string;\n}\n\nconst BADGE_CLASS: Record<NonNullable<ExtensionDetail[\"badge\"]>, string> = {\n  official: \"badge-official\",\n  popular: \"badge-popular\",\n  new: \"badge-new\",\n};\n\nconst BADGE_LABEL: Record<NonNullable<ExtensionDetail[\"badge\"]>, string> = {\n  official: \"Official\",\n  popular: \"Popular\",\n  new: \"New\",\n};\n\nconst CATEGORY_LABEL: Record<ExtensionDetail[\"category\"], string> = {\n  skills: \"Skill\",\n  mcp: \"MCP Server\",\n  slash: \"Slash Command\",\n  plugins: \"Plugin\",\n};\n\nconst CATEGORY_DOT: Record<ExtensionDetail[\"category\"], string> = {\n  skills: \"#dc7633\",\n  mcp: \"#7d6cab\",\n  slash: \"#5b8a72\",\n  plugins: \"#b08968\",\n};\n\nfunction formatCount(n: number): string {\n  if (n < 1000) return String(n);\n  return `${(n / 1000).toFixed(n >= 100000 ? 0 : 1)}k`;\n}\n\nfunction formatRelative(d: Date | null | undefined, locale: Locale): string {\n  if (!d) return \"—\";\n  const diffMs = Date.now() - d.getTime();\n  const day = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n  const days = Math.floor(diffMs / day);\n  if (days < 1) return locale === \"zh\" ? \"今天\" : \"today\";\n  if (days < 30) return locale === \"zh\" ? `${days} 天前` : `${days}d ago`;\n  const months = Math.floor(days / 30);\n  if (months < 12) return locale === \"zh\" ? `${months} 月前` : `${months}mo ago`;\n  const years = Math.floor(days / 365);\n  return locale === \"zh\" ? `${years} 年前` : `${years}y ago`;\n}\n\nexport function ExtHero({\n  ext,\n  locale = \"en\",\n  latestVersion,\n  publishedAt,\n  shareUrl,\n  shareLabel,\n  copiedLabel,\n  verifiedLabel,\n  ratingLabel,\n  downloadsLabel,\n  versionLabel,\n  updatedLabel,\n}: ExtHeroProps) {\n  const name = locale === \"zh\" && ext.nameZh ? ext.nameZh : ext.name;\n  const tagline =\n    locale === \"zh\" && ext.taglineZh ? ext.taglineZh : ext.tagline;\n  const desc =\n    locale === \"zh\" && ext.descriptionZh ? ext.descriptionZh : ext.description;\n  const blurb = tagline ?? desc ?? \"\";\n  const isMine = ext.deptId\n    ? ext.deptId === MY_DEPT_ID || ext.deptId.startsWith(`${MY_DEPT_ID}.`)\n    : false;\n  const deptTrail = ext.deptId ? deptPath(ext.deptId, locale) : null;\n  const dotColor = CATEGORY_DOT[ext.category];\n  const iconColor = ext.iconColor ?? dotColor;\n\n  return (\n    <header className=\"border-border flex flex-col gap-6 border-b pb-7 md:flex-row md:items-start md:gap-6\">\n      <div\n        className=\"flex size-20 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border-[1.5px] text-[42px] shadow-[0_6px_20px_-8px] md:size-24\"\n        style={{\n          background: `linear-gradient(135deg, ${iconColor}28 0%, ${iconColor}10 100%)`,\n          borderColor: `${iconColor}40`,\n          boxShadow: `0 6px 20px -8px ${iconColor}80`,\n        }}\n      >\n        {ext.iconEmoji}\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n        <div className=\"text-muted-foreground mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-[11.5px] font-semibold tracking-[0.08em] uppercase\">\n          <span style={{ color: dotColor }} aria-hidden>●</span>\n          <span>{CATEGORY_LABEL[ext.category]}</span>\n          {deptTrail && (\n            <>\n              <span aria-hidden>·</span>\n              <span\n                className={cn(\n                  \"inline-flex items-center gap-1 normal-case tracking-normal\",\n                  isMine ? \"text-primary\" : \"text-muted-foreground\",\n                )}\n              >\n                <Building2 className=\"size-3\" />\n                <span className=\"text-[11.5px] font-medium\">\n                  {deptTrail.join(\" / \")}\n                </span>\n              </span>\n            </>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"mb-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5\">\n          <h1 className=\"serif text-3xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] md:text-[34px]\">\n            {name}\n          </h1>\n          {ext.badge && (\n            <span\n              className={cn(\n                \"rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase\",\n                BADGE_CLASS[ext.badge],\n              )}\n            >\n              {BADGE_LABEL[ext.badge]}\n            </span>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"text-muted-foreground mb-3.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-[13px]\">\n          <span>\n            by{\" \"}\n            <span className=\"text-foreground font-mono text-[12.5px] font-semibold\">\n              {ext.slug.split(\"/\")[0] ?? ext.slug}\n            </span>\n          </span>\n          {ext.badge === \"official\" && (\n            <span className=\"text-primary inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[12px] font-semibold\">\n              <span\n                aria-hidden\n                className=\"bg-primary/15 text-primary flex size-3.5 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[9px] font-bold\"\n              >\n                ✓\n              </span>\n              {verifiedLabel}\n            </span>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        {blurb && (\n          <p className=\"text-foreground mb-4 max-w-[680px] text-[15px] leading-[1.6] text-pretty\">\n            {blurb}\n          </p>\n        )}\n\n        <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5\">\n          <InstallButton extensionId={ext.id} size=\"lg\" />\n          <SaveButton extensionId={ext.id} variant=\"pill\" />\n          <ShareButton\n            url={shareUrl}\n            label={shareLabel}\n            copiedLabel={copiedLabel}\n          />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"grid shrink-0 grid-cols-2 gap-x-7 gap-y-5 pt-1\">\n        <Stat\n          icon={<Star className=\"size-3.5\" />}\n          value={Number(ext.starsAvg).toFixed(1)}\n          label={ratingLabel}\n        />\n        <Stat\n          icon={<Download className=\"size-3.5\" />}\n          value={formatCount(ext.downloadsCount)}\n          label={downloadsLabel}\n          mono\n        />\n        <Stat\n          icon={<Tag className=\"size-3.5\" />}\n          value={latestVersion ? `v${latestVersion}` : \"—\"}\n          label={versionLabel}\n          mono\n        />\n        <Stat\n          icon={<Clock className=\"size-3.5\" />}\n          value={formatRelative(publishedAt ?? null, locale)}\n          label={updatedLabel}\n        />\n      </div>\n    </header>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Stat({\n  icon,\n  value,\n  label,\n  mono = false,\n}: {\n  icon: React.ReactNode;\n  value: string;\n  label: string;\n  mono?: boolean;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n      <div className=\"text-muted-foreground flex items-center gap-1.5\">\n        {icon}\n        <span\n          className={cn(\n            \"text-foreground text-[18px] font-bold tracking-[-0.01em]\",\n            mono && \"font-mono\",\n          )}\n        >\n          {value}\n        </span>\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"text-muted-foreground text-[10.5px] font-semibold tracking-[0.06em] uppercase\">\n        {label}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/extension/share-button.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { Check, Share2 } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\n\ninterface ShareButtonProps {\n  url: string;\n  label: string;\n  copiedLabel: string;\n}\n\nexport function ShareButton({ url, label, copiedLabel }: ShareButtonProps) {\n  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);\n\n  async function handleClick() {\n    try {\n      if (navigator.share) {\n        await navigator.share({ url });\n        return;\n      }\n    } catch {\n      // user cancelled or share unsupported — fall through to clipboard\n    }\n    try {\n      await navigator.clipboard?.writeText(url);\n      setCopied(true);\n      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1400);\n    } catch {\n      // clipboard blocked — silently ignore\n    }\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <button\n      type=\"button\"\n      onClick={handleClick}\n      className=\"border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary/50 hover:text-primary inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border bg-transparent px-3.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors\"\n    >\n      {copied ? <Check className=\"size-3.5\" /> : <Share2 className=\"size-3.5\" />}\n      {copied ? copiedLabel : label}\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "new_string": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { Folder, FolderCheck } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\nimport { useState, useTransition } from \"react\";\n\nimport { saveExtension } from \"@/lib/actions/collections\";\nimport { useRouter } from \"@/lib/i18n/navigation\";\n\ntype SaveVariant = \"icon\" | \"pill\";\n\ninterface SaveButtonProps {\n  extensionId: string;\n  variant?: SaveVariant;\n}\n\nexport function SaveButton({\n  extensionId,\n  variant = \"icon\",\n}: SaveButtonProps) {\n  const t = useTranslations(\"common\");\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();\n  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);\n\n  async function handleClick() {\n    startTransition(async () => {\n      const result = await saveExtension(extensionId);\n\n      if (!result.ok && result.error === \"unauthenticated\") {\n        router.push(\"/sign-in\");\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (result.ok) {\n        setSaved(true);\n        setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 3000);\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (variant === \"pill\") {\n    return (\n      <button\n        type=\"button\"\n        onClick={handleClick}\n        disabled={isPending}\n        className=\"border-border text-foreground hover:border-primary/50 hover:text-primary inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border bg-transparent px-3.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors disabled:opacity-60\"\n      >\n        {saved ? (\n          <FolderCheck className=\"text-primary size-3.5\" />\n        ) : (\n          <Folder className=\"size-3.5\" />\n        )}\n        {saved ? t(\"saved\") : t(\"addToGroup\")}\n      </button>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <button\n      type=\"button\"\n      onClick={handleClick}\n      disabled={isPending}\n      aria-label={saved ? t(\"saved\") : t(\"addToGroup\")}\n      className=\"bg-secondary border-border hover:bg-accent flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-md border transition-colors disabled:opacity-60\"\n    >\n      {saved ? (\n        <FolderCheck className=\"text-primary size-3.5\" />\n      ) : (\n        <Folder className=\"text-muted-foreground size-3.5\" />\n      )}\n    </button>\n  );\n}"

}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/extension/ext-tabs.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\n\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\n\nexport interface TabPanel {\n  key: string;\n  label: string;\n  content: React.ReactNode;\n}\n\ninterface ExtTabsProps {\n  tabs: TabPanel[];\n  defaultTab?: string;\n}\n\nexport function ExtTabs({ tabs, defaultTab }: ExtTabsProps) {\n  const [active, setActive] = useState(defaultTab ?? tabs[0]?.key ?? \"\");\n  const activePanel = tabs.find((t) => t.key === active) ?? tabs[0];\n\n  return (\n    <div>\n      <div\n        role=\"tablist\"\n        className=\"border-border -mx-1 mb-6 flex items-center gap-0 overflow-x-auto border-b\"\n      >\n        {tabs.map((tab) => {\n          const isActive = tab.key === active;\n          return (\n            <button\n              key={tab.key}\n              role=\"tab\"\n              type=\"button\"\n              aria-selected={isActive}\n              onClick={() => setActive(tab.key)}\n              className={cn(\n                \"-mb-px shrink-0 border-b-2 px-4.5 py-2.5 text-[13.5px] whitespace-nowrap transition-colors\",\n                isActive\n                  ? \"border-primary text-foreground font-bold\"\n                  : \"border-transparent text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground font-medium\",\n              )}\n            >\n              {tab.label}\n            </button>\n          );\n        })}\n      </div>\n      <div role=\"tabpanel\">{activePanel?.content}</div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { Globe, Github } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nimport type { ExtensionDetail } from \"@/lib/db/queries/extensions\";\nimport type { Locale } from \"@/types\";\n\ninterface ExtAboutCardProps {\n  ext: ExtensionDetail;\n  locale: Locale;\n  latestVersion?: string | null;\n  bundleSize?: bigint | null;\n  publishedAt?: Date | null;\n  labels: {\n    title: string;\n    publisher: string;\n    version: string;\n    size: string;\n    license: string;\n    updated: string;\n    scope: string;\n  };\n  scopeValue: string;\n}\n\nfunction formatBytes(b: bigint | null | undefined): string {\n  if (!b) return \"—\";\n  const n = Number(b);\n  if (n < 1024) return `${n} B`;\n  if (n < 1024 * 1024) return `${(n / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`;\n  return `${(n / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB`;\n}\n\nfunction formatRelative(d: Date | null | undefined, locale: Locale): string {\n  if (!d) return \"—\";\n  const diffMs = Date.now() - d.getTime();\n  const day = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n  const days = Math.floor(diffMs / day);\n  if (days < 1) return locale === \"zh\" ? \"今天\" : \"today\";\n  if (days < 30) return locale === \"zh\" ? `${days} 天前` : `${days}d ago`;\n  const months = Math.floor(days / 30);\n  if (months < 12) return locale === \"zh\" ? `${months} 月前` : `${months}mo ago`;\n  const years = Math.floor(days / 365);\n  return locale === \"zh\" ? `${years} 年前` : `${years}y ago`;\n}\n\nexport function ExtAboutCard({\n  ext,\n  locale,\n  latestVersion,\n  bundleSize,\n  publishedAt,\n  labels,\n  scopeValue,\n}: ExtAboutCardProps) {\n  const publisher = ext.slug.split(\"/\")[0] ?? ext.slug;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-card border-border rounded-xl border p-5\">\n      <div className=\"text-muted-foreground mb-3.5 text-[10.5px] font-bold tracking-[0.08em] uppercase\">\n        {labels.title}\n      </div>\n      <dl className=\"grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-3.5 gap-y-2.5 text-[12.5px]\">\n        <Term>{labels.publisher}</Term>\n        <Definition mono>{publisher}</Definition>\n\n        <Term>{labels.version}</Term>\n        <Definition mono>\n          {latestVersion ? `v${latestVersion}` : \"—\"}\n        </Definition>\n\n        <Term>{labels.size}</Term>\n        <Definition mono>{formatBytes(bundleSize)}</Definition>\n\n        <Term>{labels.license}</Term>\n        <Definition mono>{ext.licenseSpdx ?? \"—\"}</Definition>\n\n        <Term>{labels.updated}</Term>\n        <Definition>{formatRelative(publishedAt, locale)}</Definition>\n\n        <Term>{labels.scope}</Term>\n        <Definition bold>{scopeValue}</Definition>\n      </dl>\n\n      {(ext.homepageUrl || ext.repoUrl) && (\n        <>\n          <div className=\"bg-border my-3.5 h-px\" />\n          <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n            {ext.homepageUrl && (\n              <a\n                href={ext.homepageUrl}\n                target=\"_blank\"\n                rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n                className=\"text-foreground hover:text-primary flex items-center gap-2 py-1 text-[12.5px] transition-colors\"\n              >\n                <Globe className=\"text-muted-foreground size-3.5 shrink-0\" />\n                <span className=\"truncate font-mono text-[11.5px]\">\n                  {ext.homepageUrl.replace(/^https?:\\/\\//, \"\")}\n                </span>\n              </a>\n            )}\n            {ext.repoUrl && (\n              <a\n                href={ext.repoUrl}\n                target=\"_blank\"\n                rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n                className=\"text-foreground hover:text-primary flex items-center gap-2 py-1 text-[12.5px] transition-colors\"\n              >\n                <Github className=\"text-muted-foreground size-3.5 shrink-0\" />\n                <span className=\"truncate font-mono text-[11.5px]\">\n                  {ext.repoUrl.replace(/^https?:\\/\\//, \"\")}\n                </span>\n              </a>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Term({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  return <dt className=\"text-muted-foreground\">{children}</dt>;\n}\n\nfunction Definition({\n  children,\n  mono = false,\n  bold = false,\n}: {\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n  mono?: boolean;\n  bold?: boolean;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <dd\n      className={\n        \"text-foreground m-0 truncate\" +\n        (mono ? \" font-mono text-[11.5px]\" : \"\") +\n        (bold ? \" font-semibold\" : \"\")\n      }\n    >\n      {children}\n    </dd>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import type { RelatedExtension } from \"@/lib/db/queries/extensions\";\nimport { Link } from \"@/lib/i18n/navigation\";\nimport type { Locale } from \"@/types\";\n\ninterface ExtRelatedListProps {\n  related: RelatedExtension[];\n  locale: Locale;\n  title: string;\n}\n\nexport function ExtRelatedList({\n  related,\n  locale,\n  title,\n}: ExtRelatedListProps) {\n  if (related.length === 0) return null;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-card border-border rounded-xl border p-5\">\n      <div className=\"text-muted-foreground mb-3 text-[10.5px] font-bold tracking-[0.08em] uppercase\">\n        {title}\n      </div>\n      <ul className=\"flex flex-col\">\n        {related.map((r, i) => {\n          const name = locale === \"zh\" && r.nameZh ? r.nameZh : r.name;\n          const publisher = r.slug.split(\"/\")[0] ?? r.slug;\n          const iconColor = r.iconColor ?? \"#888\";\n          return (\n            <li\n              key={r.id}\n              className={\n                i === 0 ? \"\" : \"border-border border-t\"\n              }\n            >\n              <Link\n                href={`/extensions/${r.slug}`}\n                className=\"hover:bg-accent/50 -mx-2 flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded px-2 py-2.5 transition-colors\"\n              >\n                <div\n                  className=\"flex size-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[14px]\"\n                  style={{\n                    background: `${iconColor}1c`,\n                    borderColor: `${iconColor}33`,\n                  }}\n                >\n                  {r.iconEmoji}\n                </div>\n                <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n                  <div className=\"text-foreground truncate text-[13px] font-semibold\">\n                    {name}\n                  </div>\n                  <div className=\"text-muted-foreground truncate font-mono text-[11px]\">\n                    {publisher} · ★ {Number(r.starsAvg).toFixed(1)}\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </Link>\n            </li>\n          );\n        })}\n      </ul>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

headers } from \"next/headers\";\n\nimport { ExtAboutCard } from \"@/components/extension/ext-about-card\";\nimport { ExtHero } from \"@/components/extension/ext-hero\";\nimport { ExtRelatedList } listExtensionVersions,\n  type ExtensionDetail,\n} from \"@/lib/db/queries/extensions\";\nimport { Link } from \"@/lib/i18n/navigation\";\nimport { tagLabel } from \"@/lib/tags\";\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\nimport type { Locale } from \"@/types\";\n\ntype CompatRecord = Record<string, unknown> | null;\n\nfunction compatRows(json: CompatRecord, locale: Locale): string[] {\n  if (!json) return [];\n  return Object.entries(json).map(([key, value]) => {\n    if (value === null || value === undefined) return key;\n    if (typeof value === \"string\") return `${formatKey(key, locale)}: ${value}`;\n    if (typeof value === \"number\" || typeof value === \"boolean\") {\n      return `${formatKey(key, locale)}: ${value}`;\n    }\n    return `${formatKey(key, locale)}: ${JSON.stringify(value)}`;\n  });\n}\n\nfunction formatKey(key: string, _locale: Locale): string {\n  return key.replace(/([A-Z])/g, \" $1\").replace(/^./, (c) => c.toUpperCase());\n}\n\nexport default async function ExtensionDetailPage({\n  params,\n}: {\n  params: Promise<{ locale: string; slug: string }>;\n}) {\n  const { locale: rawLocale, slug } = await params;\n  const locale = rawLocale as Locale;\n\n  const ext = await getExtensionBySlug(slug);\n  if (!ext) notFound();\n\n  const [t, tCommon, latest, versions, related] = await Promise.all([\n    getTranslations({ locale, compatRows(\n    ext.compatibilityJson as CompatRecord,\n    locale,\n  );\n\n  const tabs: TabPanel[] = [\n    {\n      key: \"overview\",\n      label: t(\"tabs.overview\"),\n      content: <OverviewPanel ext={ext} locale={locale} t={t} />,\n    },\n    {\n      key: \"setup\",\n      label: t(\"tabs.setup\"),\n      content: (\n        <SetupPanel\n          installCmd={installCmd}\n          compatLines={compatLines}\n          tCopy={t(\"copy\")}\n          tCopied={t(\"copied\")}\n          tInstallCmd={t(\"sections.installCmd\")}\n          tRequirements={t(\"sections.requirements\")}\n          tNoRequirements={t(\"emptyRequirements\")}\n        />\n      ),\n    },\n    {\n      key: \"versions\",\n      label: `${t(\"tabs.versions\")} (${versions.length})`,\n      content: (\n        <VersionsPanel\n          versions={versions}\n          locale={locale}\n          currentLabel={t(\"currentLabel\")}\n          emptyLabel={tExt(\"noVersions\")}\n        />\n      ),\n    },\n    {\n      key: \"reviews\",\n      label: t(\"tabs.reviews\"),\n      content: (\n        <EmptyPanel\n          icon=\"reviews\"\n          message={t(\"emptyReviews\")}\n          ctaLabel={t(\"writeReview\")}\n        />\n      ),\n    },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-[1180px] px-7 py-6\">\n      <Link\n        href=\"/extensions\"\n        className=\"text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-accent -ml-2 mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors\"\n      >\n        <ChevronLeft className=\"size-3.5\" />\n        {t(\"back\")}\n      </Link>\n\n      <ExtHero\n        ext={ext}\n        locale={locale}\n        latestVersion={latest?.version ?? null}\n        publishedAt={latest?.publishedAt ?? ext.publishedAt ?? null}\n        shareUrl={shareUrl}\n        shareLabel={t(\"share\")}\n        copiedLabel={t(\"copied\")}\n        verifiedLabel={t(\"verified\")}\n        ratingLabel={t(\"stats.rating\")}\n        downloadsLabel={t(\"stats.downloads\")}\n        versionLabel={t(\"stats.version\")}\n        updatedLabel={t(\"stats.updated\")}\n      />\n\n      <div className=\"mt-7 grid gap-9 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_320px]\">\n        <main className=\"min-w-0\">\n          <ExtTabs tabs={tabs} />\n        </main>\n\n        <aside className=\"flex flex-col gap-4 lg:sticky lg:top-5 lg:self-start\">\n          <ExtAboutCard\n            ext={ext}\n            locale={locale}\n            latestVersion={latest?.version ?? null}\n            bundleSize={latest?.bundleSize ?? null}\n            publishedAt={latest?.publishedAt ?? ext.publishedAt ?? null}\n            labels={{\n              title: t(\"about\"),\n              publisher: t(\"publisher\"),\n              version: t(\"stats.version\"),\n              size: t(\"size\"),\n              license: tMetadata(\"license\"),\n              updated: t(\"stats.updated\"),\n              scope: tMetadata(\"scope\"),\n            }}\n            scopeValue={tMetadata(`scopeValues.${ext.scope}`)}\n          />\n          <ExtRelatedList\n            related={related}\n            locale={locale}\n            title={t(\"related\")}\n          />\n        </aside>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction OverviewPanel({\n  ext,\n  locale,\n  t,\n}: {\n  ext: ExtensionDetail;\n  locale: Locale;\n  t: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof (\n        <MarkdownContent>{ext.readmeMd}</MarkdownContent>\n      ) : (\n        <p className=\"text-muted-foreground py-12 text-center text-[13px]\">\n          {t(\"emptyReadme\")}\n        </p>\n      )}\n\n      {ext.tagIds.length > 0 && (\n        <section>\n          <h2 className=\"serif mb-3 text-[18px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.01em]\">\n            {t(\"sections.tags\")}\n          </h2>\n          <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1.5\">\n            {ext.tagIds.map((tag) => (\n              <Link\n                key={tag}\n                href={`/extensions?tags=${encodeURIComponent(tag)}&dept=__all`}\n                className=\"border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary hover:bg-primary/10 rounded border bg-transparent px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-[12px] font-semibold transition-colors\"\n              >\n                #{tagLabel(tag, locale)}\n              </Link>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </section>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction SetupPanel({\n  installCmd,\n  compatLines,\n  tCopy,\n  tCopied,\n  tInstallCmd,\n  tRequirements,\n  tNoRequirements,\n}: {\n  installCmd: string;\n  compatLines: string[];\n  tCopy: string;\n  tCopied: string;\n  tInstallCmd: string;\n  tRequirements: string;\n  tNoRequirements: string;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-7\">\n      <section>\n        <h2 className=\"serif mb-3.5 text-[20px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.01em]\">\n          {tInstallCmd}\n        </h2>\n        <InstallCommand\n          command={installCmd}\n          copyLabel={tCopy}\n          copiedLabel={tCopied}\n        />\n      </section>\n\n      <section>\n        <h2 className=\"serif mb-3.5 text-[20px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.01em]\">\n          {tRequirements}\n        </h2>\n        {compatLines.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"text-muted-foreground text-[13px]\">\n            {tNoRequirements}\n          </p>\n        ) : (\n          <ul className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n            {compatLines.map((line, i) => (\n              <li\n                key={i}\n                className=\"bg-secondary border-border flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2.5\"\n              >\n                <Check className=\"text-primary size-3.5 shrink-0\" />\n                <span className=\"font-mono text-[12.5px]\">{line}</span>\n              </li>\n            ))}\n          </ul>\n        )}\n      </section>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction VersionsPanel({\n  versions,\n  locale,\n  currentLabel,\n  emptyLabel,\n}: {\n  versions: Array<{\n    version: string;\n    changelog: string | null;\n    changelogZh: string | null;\n    publishedAt: Date | null;\n  }>;\n  locale: Locale;\n  currentLabel: string;\n  emptyLabel: string;\n}) {\n  if (versions.length === 0) {\n    return (\n      <p className=\"text-muted-foreground py-8 text-center text-[13px]\">\n        {emptyLabel}\n      </p>\n    );\n  }\n  const fmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale === \"zh\" ? \"zh-CN\" : \"en\", {\n    year: \"numeric\",\n    month: \"short\",\n    day: \"numeric\",\n  });\n  return (\n    <ul className=\"flex flex-col\">\n      {versions.map((v, i) => {\n        const note =\n          locale === \"zh\" && v.changelogZh\n            ? v.changelogZh\n            : (v.changelog ?? \"\");\n        return (\n          <li\n            key={`${v.version}-${i}`}\n            className={cn(\n              \"flex gap-6 py-5\",\n              i === 0 ? \"\" : \"border-border border-t\",\n            )}\n          >\n            <div className=\"w-[120px] shrink-0\">\n              <div\n                className={cn(\n                  \"font-mono text-[14px] font-bold\",\n                  i === 0 ? \"text-primary\" : \"text-foreground\",\n                )}\n              >\n                v{v.version}\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"text-muted-foreground mt-1 text-[11.5px]\">\n                {v.publishedAt ? fmt.format(v.publishedAt) : \"\"}\n              </div>\n              {i === 0 && (\n                <div className=\"text-primary mt-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[0.08em] uppercase\">\n                  {currentLabel}\n                </div>\n              )}\n            </div>\n            <p className=\"text-foreground m-0 flex-1 text-[14px] leading-[1.6] text-pretty\">\n              {note}\n            </p>\n          </li>\n        );\n      })}\n    </ul>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction EmptyPanel({\n  icon,\n  message,\n  ctaLabel,\n}: {\n  icon: \"reviews\";\n  message: string;\n  ctaLabel: string;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"border-border bg-card/40 flex flex-col items-center gap-4 rounded-lg border border-dashed py-12 text-center\">\n      <TagIcon className=\"text-muted-foreground/60 size-7\" />\n      <p className=\"text-muted-foreground max-w-sm text-[13.5px]\">{message}</p>\n      <button\n        type=\"button\"\n        className=\"border-border text-foreground hover:border-primary/50 hover:text-primary rounded-md border px-3.5 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-semibold transition-colors\"\n        // Reviews submission flow not implemented yet — disabled placeholder.\n        disabled\n      >\n        + {ctaLabel}\n      </button>\n      <span className=\"sr-only\">{icon}</span>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport async function generateMetadata({\n  params,\n}: {\n  params: Promise<{ locale: string; slug: string }>;\n}) {\n  const { locale, slug } = await params;\n  const ext = await getExtensionBySlug(slug);\n  if (!ext) return { title: \"Not found · AgentCenter\" };\n  const name = locale === \"zh\" && ext.nameZh ? ext.nameZh : ext.name;\n  return {\n    title: `${name} · AgentCenter`,\n    description: ext.tagline ?? ext.description ?? undefined,\n  };\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

All green: lint, typecheck, and 182 tests pass. The page renders at `/{en,zh}/extensions/web-search-pro` with all the redesigned copy ("Back to marketplace", "Verified publisher", "Install via CLI", "Related extensions", four tabs, etc.).

Summary of what changed to match the new AgentCenter.html design:

**New components**
- `components/extension/share-button.tsx` — share button with copy fallback
- `components/extension/install-command.tsx` — terminal-style install snippet with copy
- `components/extension/ext-tabs.tsx` — client tab container
- `components/extension/ext-about-card.tsx` — refreshed sidebar "About" card with publisher/version/size/license/updated/scope + homepage/repo links
- `components/extension/ext-related-list.tsx` — related-extensions sidebar card

**Reworked**
- `components/extension/ext-hero.tsx` — bigger icon block, category breadcrumb, badge + verified tag, tagline/description, Install/Save/Share CTA row, four-stat grid (rating/downloads/version/updated)
- `components/extension/save-button.tsx` — added a `pill` variant for the hero CTA row
- `app/[locale]/extensions/[slug]/page.tsx` — back link, new hero, tabbed body (Overview / Setup / Versions / Reviews) with sticky sidebar
- `app/[locale]/extensions/[slug]/loading.tsx` — skeleton matches the new layout
- `lib/db/queries/extensions.ts` — added `getLatestExtensionVersion`, `listExtensionVersions`, `getRelatedExtensions`
- `lib/i18n/messages/{en,zh}.json` — new `detail` namespace

**Removed**
- `components/extension/ext-metadata-panel.tsx` — replaced by the new About card

Tab content: Overview = README + tags chips; Setup = `agentcenter install <slug>` block + requirements derived from `compatibilityJson`; Versions = real changelog from `extension_versions`; Reviews = empty-state placeholder (no reviews data yet). Permissions tab from the design was dropped since there's no permissions data in the schema; if/when it lands we can add it back as a fifth tab.

> DEVELOPER

what's next?

> AGENT

A few options, in rough priority order:

1. **Land the work** — these changes are uncommitted on `main`, but per `CLAUDE.md` we always work on a feature branch + PR. I can spin up `feat/detail-page-redesign`, commit, push, and open the PR for review.
2. **Tests for the new pieces** — `share-button`, `install-command`, and `ext-tabs` have no coverage; the existing button tests are a clean template to copy.
3. **Permissions tab** — dropped because the schema doesn't carry permission data yet. If you want it back, we'd need to extend the manifest spec + `extensions` schema, then plug in the panel that's already designed.

My recommendation: do (1) first so this isn't sitting in your worktree, then decide between (2) and (3). Want me to open the PR?

import { defineConfig } from "vitest/config";
import { resolve } from "path";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
  test: {
    // Default environment for lib/ and cli/ unit tests.
    // components/ tests carry a `// @vitest-environment happy-dom` pragma.
    environment: "node",
    setupFiles: ["./vitest.setup.ts"],
    // Unit tests live next to their source files in lib/ and cli/.
    // components/ client components are tested here too.
    // app/ RSC pages and routes are covered by tests/e2e/ (Playwright).
    include: ["lib/**/*.test.ts", "cli/**/*.test.ts", "components/**/*.test.tsx"],
    env: {
      // Prevents neon() from throwing at import time in unit tests.
      // No real DB connection is made — queries are never executed.
      DATABASE_URL: "REDACTED",
    },
    coverage: {
      provider: "v8",
      include: ["lib/**/*.ts", "cli/**/*.ts"],
      exclude: ["lib/db/schema/**", "lib/jobs/client.ts", "**/*.test.ts"],
    },
  },
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      "@": resolve(__dirname, "."),
    },
  },
});
```

> AGENT

Vitest covers `components/`, `lib/`, `cli/` only — server components and pages go through Playwright e2e (which isn't part of `bun test`). DB queries don't run in unit tests (no real DB connection). That narrows the surface.

Here's what I can meaningfully cover in vitest, prioritized:

**High value — new client behavior**
1. `ExtTabs` — clicking a non-default tab swaps the visible panel; default tab renders first
2. `InstallCommand` — Copy button writes the command to the clipboard and shows "Copied"
3. `ShareButton` — falls back to clipboard when `navigator.share` is undefined; shows "Copied"
4. `SaveButton` pill variant — renders the label text (icon variant already tested)

**Medium — only worth it if you want belt-and-braces**
5. `InstallCommand`/`ShareButton` — "Copied" reverts after the timeout

**Out of scope for vitest in this project**
- Page rendering (404, default tab, Overview content) — Playwright territory
- DB queries (`getLatestExtensionVersion`, etc.) — no DB in unit tests
- Hero/AboutCard/RelatedList rendering — server components; smoke-rendering server components in vitest is fragile and the value is low

My recommendation: do (1)–(4). That's four small files, each one clear behavior. Skip (5) — testing setTimeout/timer behavior tends to be brittle.

OK to proceed with (1)–(4)?

  "content": "// @vitest-environment happy-dom\nimport { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from \"vitest\";\nimport { cleanup, render, screen, waitFor } from \"@testing-library/react\";\nimport userEvent from \"@testing-library/user-event\";\n\nimport { InstallCommand } from \"./install-command\";\n\nconst writeText = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);\n\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  writeText.mockClear();\n  Object.assign(navigator, { clipboard: { writeText } });\n});\nafterEach(cleanup);\n\ndescribe(\"InstallCommand\", () => {\n  it(\"renders the command with a $ prompt\", () => {\n    render(\n      <InstallCommand\n        command=\"agentcenter install foo\"\n        copyLabel=\"Copy\"\n        copiedLabel=\"Copied\"\n      />,\n    );\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"agentcenter install foo\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"$\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it(\"writes the command to the clipboard when Copy is clicked\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(\n      <InstallCommand\n        command=\"agentcenter install foo\"\n        copyLabel=\"Copy\"\n        copiedLabel=\"Copied\"\n      />,\n    );\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"Copy\" }));\n\n    expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\"agentcenter install foo\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"flips the button label to Copied after a successful copy\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(\n      <InstallCommand\n        command=\"agentcenter install foo\"\n        copyLabel=\"Copy\"\n        copiedLabel=\"Copied\"\n      />,\n    );\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"Copy\" }));\n\n    await waitFor(() =>\n      expect(\n        screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: /Copied/ }),\n      ).toBeInTheDocument(),\n    );\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/extension/share-button.test.tsx",
  "content": "// @vitest-environment happy-dom\nimport { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\nimport { cleanup, render, screen, waitFor } from \"@testing-library/react\";\nimport userEvent from \"@testing-library/user-event\";\n\nimport { ShareButton } from \"./share-button\";\n\nafterEach(cleanup);\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  // Force the clipboard fallback path by ensuring navigator.share is absent.\n  if (\"share\" in navigator) {\n    Object.defineProperty(navigator, \"share\", {\n      value: undefined,\n      configurable: true,\n    });\n  }\n});\n\ndescribe(\"ShareButton\", () => {\n  it(\"renders the provided label\", () => {\n    render(\n      <ShareButton url=\"https://x\" label=\"Share\" copiedLabel=\"Copied\" />,\n    );\n    expect(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"Share\" })).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it(\"copies the URL and flips the label when Web Share is unavailable\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(\n      <ShareButton\n        url=\"https://example.com/ext/foo\"\n        label=\"Share\"\n        copiedLabel=\"Copied\"\n      />,\n    );\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"Share\" }));\n\n    await waitFor(() =>\n      expect(\n        screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: /Copied/ }),\n      ).toBeInTheDocument(),\n    );\n    await expect(navigator.clipboard.readText()).resolves.toBe(\n      \"https://example.com/ext/foo\",\n    );\n  });\n});\n"
}

test(detail): cover new client components from the detail-page redesign

Adds vitest coverage for the four new/updated client components on
the redesigned extension detail page:

- ExtTabs: default panel, click-to-swap, defaultTab override

  clipboard contents and the "Copied" label flip)
- ShareButton: same copy-and-flip path through the navigator.share
  fallback
- SaveButton (pill variant): label visibility and post-save state

DB queries and the page itself are unchanged scope — those are
Playwright territory in this repo's vitest config.
```

> AGENT

Here's the full CodeRabbit triage for PR #12 — 6 inline comments:

| # | File:Line | Severity | Issue |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `page.tsx:67` | Major | `x-forwarded-host`/`host` headers are spoofable → share URL could point anywhere. Use a trusted public base URL or host allowlist. |
| 2 | `ext-about-card.tsx:112` | Major | `homepageUrl`/`repoUrl` come from extension manifests → should validate the URL parses and is `http:`/`https:` before rendering in `href`, else `javascript:` URIs sneak through. |
| 3 | `ext-tabs.tsx:49` | Major | The manual `role="tab"` markup is click-only, no keyboard nav (arrow/Home/End), no `aria-controls`. Project already uses `@base-ui/react` (see `select.tsx`). Suggests using `@base-ui/react/tabs`. |
| 4 | `install-command.tsx:22` | Major | `await navigator.clipboard?.writeText(...)` evaluates to `await undefined` if clipboard is missing, which resolves successfully — so we flip to "Copied" without actually copying. Guard before awaiting. |
| 5 | `install-command.tsx:41` | Major | Hard-coded zinc/hex values for the terminal block. Suggests replacing with theme tokens. |
| 6 | `share-button.tsx:31` | Minor | If user cancels the native share sheet on mobile (`AbortError`), the `catch` falls through to `clipboard.writeText` and shows "Copied" — user opted out of sharing but their clipboard was overwritten anyway. |

**My take on each:**

- **(1), (2), (4)** are real correctness/security bugs worth fixing — small changes.
- **(6)** is a real UX bug, also small.
- **(3)** is legitimate accessibility work; modest effort, fits the project's Base UI pattern.
- **(5)** I'd push back: the dark terminal block is intentional (the original `AgentCenter.html` design also uses dark in both themes — terminals are conventionally dark even in light UIs). Worth a reply explaining why we're keeping it.

Recommend fixing 1, 2, 4, 6, and 3, and replying to dismiss 5. Want me to do that?

  "new_string": "import { clsx, type ClassValue } from \"clsx\"\nimport { twMerge } from \"tailwind-merge\"\n\nexport function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {\n  return twMerge(clsx(inputs))\n}\n\n/**\n * Returns the URL only when it parses and uses an http(s) scheme.\n * Use for any href whose value comes from extension manifests / user input,\n * to keep `javascript:`, `data:`, etc. out of the rendered DOM.\n */\nexport function safeExternalUrl(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {\n  if (!value) return null;\n  try {\n    const url = new URL(value);\n    return url.protocol === \"http:\" || url.protocol === \"https:\"\n      ? url.toString()\n      : null;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}"
}

**Fix 4 — install-command guards `writeText`:**

> TOOL

id: toolu_013ndUhpQVZBmU3wzc52khZA
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/extension/install-command.tsx",
  "old_string": "  async function handleCopy() {\n    try {\n      await navigator.clipboard?.writeText(command);\n      setCopied(true);\n      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1400);\n    } catch {\n      // clipboard blocked\n    }\n  }",
  "new_string": "  async function handleCopy() {\n    // `await undefined` resolves successfully, so `?.writeText(...)` would\n    // flip the label to Copied even when no copy actually happened. Bail\n    // early when the API isn't there.\n    if (typeof navigator.clipboard?.writeText !== \"function\") return;\n    try {\n      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(command);\n      setCopied(true);\n      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1400);\n    } catch {\n      // clipboard blocked\n    }\n  }"
}
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/extension/share-button.tsx",
  "old_string": "  async function handleClick() {\n    try {\n      if (navigator.share) {\n        await navigator.share({ url });\n        return;\n      }\n    } catch {\n      // user cancelled or share unsupported — fall through to clipboard\n    }\n    try {\n      await navigator.clipboard?.writeText(url);\n      setCopied(true);\n      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1400);\n    } catch {\n      // clipboard blocked — silently ignore\n    }\n  }",
  "new_string": "  async function handleClick() {\n    // Use the native share sheet when the platform exposes it. Any error from\n    // share() (including AbortError when the user dismisses the sheet) means\n    // the user has already seen and acted on the share UI — don't silently\n    // overwrite their clipboard as a \"fallback\".\n    if (typeof navigator.share === \"function\") {\n      try {\n        await navigator.share({ url });\n      } catch {\n        // user cancelled or share rejected — stop here\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (typeof navigator.clipboard?.writeText !== \"function\") return;\n    try {\n      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);\n      setCopied(true);\n      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1400);\n    } catch {\n      // clipboard blocked — silently ignore\n    }\n  }"
}

  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { Tabs } from \"@base-ui/react/tabs\";\n\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\n\nexport interface TabPanel {\n  key: string;\n  label: string;\n  content: React.ReactNode;\n}\n\ninterface ExtTabsProps {\n  tabs: TabPanel[];\n  defaultTab?: string;\n}\n\nexport function ExtTabs({ tabs, defaultTab }: ExtTabsProps) {\n  if (tabs.length === 0) return null;\n\n  return (\n    <Tabs.Root defaultValue={defaultTab ?? tabs[0]!.key}>\n      <Tabs.List className=\"border-border -mx-1 mb-6 flex items-center gap-0 overflow-x-auto border-b\">\n        {tabs.map((tab) => (\n          <Tabs.Tab\n            key={tab.key}\n            value={tab.key}\n            className={cn(\n              \"-mb-px shrink-0 border-b-2 border-transparent px-4.5 py-2.5 text-[13.5px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap\",\n              \"text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground transition-colors\",\n              \"data-[selected]:border-primary data-[selected]:text-foreground data-[selected]:font-bold\",\n              \"focus-visible:outline-ring focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2\",\n            )}\n          >\n            {tab.label}\n          </Tabs.Tab>\n        ))}\n      </Tabs.List>\n      {tabs.map((tab) => (\n        <Tabs.Panel\n          key={tab.key}\n          value={tab.key}\n          className=\"focus-visible:outline-ring focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2\"\n        >\n          {tab.content}\n        </Tabs.Panel>\n      ))}\n    </Tabs.Root>\n  );\n}\n"

> AGENT

**Proposed commit:**

```
fix(detail): address CodeRabbit security and a11y feedback on PR #12

- Build the share URL from a trusted NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL instead of
  request headers (x-forwarded-host is spoofable when the edge layer
  doesn't rewrite it).
- Allowlist http(s) on extension-supplied homepage/repo URLs before
  rendering them in href via a new safeExternalUrl helper, so
  javascript:/data: URIs can't reach the DOM.
- Replace the hand-rolled tablist in ExtTabs with @base-ui/react/tabs
  to get keyboard navigation (arrow / Home / End), aria-controls
  wiring, and selected-state styling for free. Public API of ExtTabs
  is unchanged.
- Guard navigator.clipboard?.writeText before awaiting it in
  InstallCommand — `await undefined` was resolving successfully and
  flipping the label to "Copied" without an actual write.
- Stop falling through to clipboard.writeText when navigator.share()
  rejects in ShareButton: an AbortError on mobile means the user
  cancelled the share sheet, not that the API is missing.

Tests: keep ExtTabs coverage and add an ARIA-wiring assertion
(aria-selected, aria-controls, data-orientation). The arrow-key
nav itself is Base UI's contract; happy-dom can't simulate the
focus-key chain it depends on, so we don't try to reproduce it
in vitest.
```

OK to commit?

Public API of ExtTabs\n  is unchanged.\n- Guard navigator.clipboard?.writeText before awaiting it in\n  InstallCommand — `await undefined` was resolving successfully and\n  flipping the label to \"Copied\" without an actual write.\n- Stop falling through to clipboard.writeText when navigator.share()\n  rejects in ShareButton: an AbortError on mobile means the user\n  cancelled the share sheet, not that the API is missing.\n\nTests: keep ExtTabs coverage and add an ARIA-wiring assertion\n(aria-selected, aria-controls, data-orientation). The arrow-key\nnav itself is Base UI's contract; happy-dom can't simulate the\nfocus-key chain it depends on, so we don't try to reproduce it\nin vitest.\nEOF\n)\" && git status",

> AGENT

**Proposed commit:**

```
fix(detail): drop sr-only icon token leaking to screen readers

EmptyPanel rendered <span className="sr-only">{icon}</span>, which
read out the literal string "reviews" with no surrounding context
to assistive tech. The prop wasn't actually controlling anything —
the TagIcon next to it is hard-coded — so drop the prop entirely
and mark the icon aria-hidden. The visible message already carries
the meaning for AT.
```

OK to commit and push?
