1	import { and, desc, eq, ilike, inArray, like, or, type SQL, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
2	
3	import { MY_DEPT_ID } from "@/lib/data/departments"; // fallback for unauthenticated users
4	import { db } from "@/lib/db/client";
5	import { extensions, extensionTags } from "@/lib/db/schema";
6	import type { Filters } from "@/lib/validators/filters";
7	
8	// Special token in the dept URL param meaning "show all departments".
9	const ALL_DEPTS = "__all";
10	
11	/**
12	 * Compose a SQL WHERE expression from typed filters. Returns `undefined` when
13	 * no clauses are active so callers can omit `.where()` entirely.
14	 */
15	export function buildExtensionWhere(
16	  filters: Filters,
17	  fallbackDeptId?: string,
18	): SQL | undefined {
19	  const clauses = [
20	    // Always limit to published extensions.
21	    eq(extensions.visibility, "published"),
22	
23	    filters.category ? eq(extensions.category, filters.category) : undefined,
24	    filters.scope ? eq(extensions.scope, filters.scope) : undefined,
25	    filters.funcCat ? eq(extensions.funcCat, filters.funcCat) : undefined,
26	    filters.subCat ? eq(extensions.subCat, filters.subCat) : undefined,
27	    filters.l2 ? eq(extensions.l2, filters.l2) : undefined,
28	
29	    // Dotted-path descendant filter — uses idx_ext_dept_path (text_pattern_ops).
30	    // When no dept param is provided, fall back to the authenticated user's
31	    // defaultDeptId (passed in from the page via getSession), then to MY_DEPT_ID
32	    // for unauthenticated visitors.
33	    (() => {
34	      const dept = filters.dept ?? fallbackDeptId ?? MY_DEPT_ID;
35	      if (dept === ALL_DEPTS) return undefined;
36	      return or(
37	        eq(extensions.deptId, dept),
38	        like(extensions.deptId, `${dept}.%`),
39	      );
40	    })(),
41	
42	    // Filter chips
43	    filters.filter === "trending"
44	      ? sql`${extensions.downloadsCount} > 50000`
45	      : undefined,
46	    filters.filter === "new" ? eq(extensions.badge, "new") : undefined,
47	    filters.filter === "official"
48	      ? eq(extensions.badge, "official")
49	      : undefined,
50	    filters.filter === "free"
51	      ? sql`${extensions.licenseSpdx} IS NOT NULL`
52	      : undefined,
53	
54	    // FTS via tsvector (set weight A/B/C in the GENERATED column) with
55	    // pg_trgm ILIKE fallback for short queries and CJK substrings.
56	    filters.q
57	      ? or(
58	          sql`${extensions.searchVector} @@ websearch_to_tsquery('simple', ${filters.q})`,
59	          ilike(extensions.name, `%${filters.q}%`),
60	          ilike(extensions.nameZh, `%${filters.q}%`),
61	        )
62	      : undefined,
63	
64	    // Tag filter via aggregating subquery — `any` matches if at least one tag
65	    // overlaps; `all` requires every selected tag to be present.
66	    filters.tags && filters.tags.length > 0
67	      ? inArray(
68	          extensions.id,
69	          db
70	            .select({ id: extensionTags.extensionId })
71	            .from(extensionTags)
72	            .where(inArray(extensionTags.tagId, filters.tags))
73	            .groupBy(extensionTags.extensionId)
74	            .having(
75	              filters.tagMatch === "all"
76	                ? sql`count(*) = ${filters.tags.length}`
77	                : sql`count(*) >= 1`,
78	            ),
79	        )
80	      : undefined,
81	  ].filter((c): c is SQL => Boolean(c));
82	
83	  return clauses.length > 0 ? and(...clauses) : undefined;
84	}
85	
86	export function buildExtensionOrder(sort: Filters["sort"]) {
87	  switch (sort) {
88	    case "stars":
89	      return [desc(extensions.starsAvg), desc(extensions.downloadsCount)];
90	    case "recent":
91	      return [desc(extensions.publishedAt), desc(extensions.createdAt)];
92	    case "downloads":
93	    default:
94	      return [desc(extensions.downloadsCount)];
95	  }
96	}
97

> AGENT

Reproduced and diagnosed — the earlier "Failed query" was actually a transient Neon HTTP fetch failure, not a code bug:

```
NeonDbError: Error connecting to database: TypeError: fetch failed
params: published, eng.cloud.infra, eng.cloud.infra.%, 24
```

The query parameters are fine; Neon's serverless driver just lost a network round-trip. The listing returns 200 with real data right now — no code change needed.

I jumped on it earlier because the error overlay made it look like a query construction issue. False alarm. Sorry for the misdirection.

**What's actually next** — three real options:

1. **Look at `docs/plan.md`** to see what phase is queued after the detail-page redesign.
2. **Harden the listing for Neon transient failures** — wrap `listExtensions`/`countFilteredExtensions` so the page renders an empty state with a "try again" instead of a blown-up React error overlay. Modest scope, real value if Neon flaps.
3. **Stop here** — wrap up the session.

Which way?

> DEVELOPER

harden the listing for transient failures

> AGENT

Let me check the existing error-boundary setup before designing the fix.

1	import { getTranslations } from "next-intl/server";
2	
3	import { ExtGrid } from "@/components/extension/ext-grid";
4	import { FilterBar } from "@/components/filters/filter-bar";
5	import { getSession } from "@/lib/auth/session";
6	import {
7	  countFilteredExtensions,
8	  listExtensions,
9	} from "@/lib/db/queries/extensions";
10	import { getTagsWithCounts } from "@/lib/db/queries/tags";
11	import { parseFilters } from "@/lib/validators/filters";
12	import type { Locale } from "@/types";
13	
14	export default async function ExtensionsPage({
15	  params,
16	  searchParams,
17	}: {
18	  params: Promise<{ locale: string }>;
19	  searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;
20	}) {
21	  const [{ locale }, rawParams, session] = await Promise.all([
22	    params,
23	    searchParams,
24	    getSession(),
25	  ]);
26	  const t = await getTranslations("extensions");
27	  const filters = parseFilters(rawParams);
28	  const userDeptId = session?.user.defaultDeptId ?? undefined;
29	
30	  const [items, total, tags] = await Promise.all([
31	    listExtensions(filters, userDeptId),
32	    countFilteredExtensions(filters, userDeptId),
33	    getTagsWithCounts(),
34	  ]);
35	
36	  return (
37	    <div className="px-7 py-5">
38	      <header className="mb-5 flex items-baseline gap-3">
39	        <h1 className="serif text-2xl tracking-tight">{t("browseTitle")}</h1>
40	        <span className="text-muted-foreground text-[13px]">
41	          {t("count", { count: total })}
42	        </span>
43	      </header>
44	
45	      <FilterBar tags={tags} />
46	
47	      <ExtGrid items={items} locale={locale as Locale} query={filters.q} />
48	    </div>
49	  );
50	}

> AGENT

Here's the plan before I start. The current state:

- `app/[locale]/error.tsx` already exists, but it dumps `error.message` straight to the page — which means users see raw SQL on a Neon failure (a leak of internals).
- The listing page doesn't have its own error boundary, so it falls back to the locale one.

Two targeted changes:

1. **New `app/[locale]/extensions/error.tsx`** — listing-specific boundary with a friendly "Couldn't load extensions, try again" empty state and a retry button (calls `reset()`). Sidebar + topbar from the layout still render normally.
2. **Update `app/[locale]/error.tsx`** — stop rendering `error.message` (which can include SQL/internals); show a generic message + digest only. Stays a useful catchall.

I'd also add i18n keys for both messages and one focused test on the new boundary's retry behavior.

What I'm **not** doing: retry-with-backoff, specific Neon error detection, or wrapping each query in try/catch. Error boundaries are simpler and cover all DB calls uniformly.

Sound right?

> AGENT

**Files changing:**
- `app/[locale]/extensions/error.tsx` *(new)* — listing-specific boundary, friendly empty state, retry button, surfaces digest only
- `app/[locale]/error.tsx` — stop rendering raw `error.message` (would leak SQL fragments and internals); use generic copy + digest, switch to i18n
- `lib/i18n/messages/{en,zh}.json` — add `extensions.errorBoundary.*` and `errors.generic.*` keys

**Proposed commit:**

```
fix(error): contain transient DB failures and stop leaking error.message

The listing relied on the locale-level error boundary, which rendered
error.message verbatim — meaning a Neon HTTP fetch failure surfaced
the raw SQL and stack to users.

- Add a listing-specific error.tsx that shows a friendly "couldn't
  load extensions" state with a retry button. The locale layout
  (sidebar + topbar) keeps rendering, so users can navigate away.
- Sanitize the locale error.tsx: drop error.message in favour of a
  generic copy and the opaque error.digest for support correlation.
  Switch both boundaries to next-intl strings.

No retry-with-backoff or per-query try/catch — the boundary uniformly
covers every server-component throw inside the route, which is what
matters for transient Neon blips.
```

OK to commit and push?

## Walkthrough

Two error boundary UI components are enhanced or created to use `next-intl` translations. The locale error boundary is updated to localize all user-facing text and log with a prefix. A new extensions error boundary component is introduced following the same localized pattern. Supporting translation strings are added in English and Chinese for both error scenarios.

## Changes

**Localized Error Boundary Components**

| Layer / File(s) | Summary |
|---|---|
| **Translation Definitions** <br> `lib/i18n/messages/en.json`, `lib/i18n/messages/zh.json` | `extensions.errorBoundary` object is added with `title`, `body`, and `retry` strings. `extensions.noVersions` message is expanded in both English and Chinese. |
| **Locale-Level Error Boundary** <br> `app/[locale]/error.tsx` | Component now uses `useTranslations("errors.generic")` to localize error title, body, and retry button text. Error logging includes `[locale-error-boundary]` prefix. `error.digest` is conditionally rendered in a monospace code block. Button and icon styling are updated. |
| **Extensions Error Boundary Component** <br> `app/[locale]/extensions/error.tsx` | New client component `ExtensionsListingError` is created with a typed `Props` interface. Uses `useTranslations("extensions.errorBoundary")` for localized text, logs errors on mount, renders an alert card with retry button, and optionally displays `error.digest`. |

## Estimated code review effort

🎯 2 (Simple) | ⏱️ ~12 minutes

## Poem

> 🐰 Two error guards now speak in many tongues,  
> With digest codes for debugging wrongs,  
> Locale and extensions, both retry-bound,

> AGENT

**Files:**
- `app/[locale]/extensions/[slug]/error.tsx` *(new)* — detail-page error boundary; shows friendly empty state with **Retry** + **Back to marketplace** (extra escape hatch since users may not want to retry the same extension), surfaces digest only
- `lib/i18n/messages/{en,zh}.json` — `detail.errorBoundary.*` keys

**Proposed commit:**

```
fix(detail): add error boundary for the extension detail route

Mirror PR #13's listing boundary on the detail page so a transient
DB failure (or any throw in getExtensionBySlug / latest-version /
related-extensions queries) renders a friendly state instead of
falling through to the locale boundary.

Adds a Back to marketplace link alongside Retry — users may not
want to retry the same broken slug. Reuses the same digest-only
disclosure pattern.
```

OK to commit, push, open PR?

import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { getTranslations } from "next-intl/server";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";

import { getSession } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { getMyExtensions } from "@/lib/actions/publish";
import { Link } from "@/lib/i18n/navigation";

export async function generateMetadata() {
  const t = await getTranslations("publish.dashboard");
  return { title: t("title") };
}

export default async function PublishDashboardPage() {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session) redirect("/sign-in");

  const t = await getTranslations("publish.dashboard");
  const exts = await getMyExtensions(session.user.id);

  const statusLabel: Record<string, string> = {
    draft: t("statusDraft"),
    published: t("statusPublished"),
    archived: t("statusArchived"),
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-10">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold">{t("title")}</h1>
        <Link
          href="/publish/new"
          className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity"
        >
          <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
          {t("newButton")}
        </Link>
      </div>

      {exts.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border p-16 text-center text-muted-foreground">
          {t("empty")}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <ul className="space-y-3">
          {exts.map((ext) => (
            <li
              key={ext.id}
              className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card px-5 py-4"
            >
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">
                <span className="font-medium">{ext.name}</span>
                <span className="ml-2 font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">{ext.slug}</span>
              </div>
              <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full border border-border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {statusLabel[ext.visibility] ?? ext.visibility}
              </span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
```

import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { getTranslations } from "next-intl/server";

import { ExtGrid } from "@/components/extension/ext-grid";
import { Link } from "@/lib/i18n/navigation";
import {
  countPublishedExtensions,
  getFeaturedExtension,
  listExtensions,
} from "@/lib/db/queries/extensions";
import type { Locale } from "@/types";

const TRENDING_LIMIT = 8;

export default async function Home({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ locale: string }>;
}) {
  const { locale } = await params;
  const t = await getTranslations("home");

  const [featured, totalCount, trending] = await Promise.all([
    getFeaturedExtension(),
    countPublishedExtensions(),
    listExtensions({ category: "skills", sort: "downloads", dept: "__all" }),
  ]);

  return (
    <div className="px-7 py-5">
      {featured && (
        <section className="bg-card border-border relative mb-7 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border p-9 shadow-[0_1px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.02)]">
          <div
            aria-hidden
            className="bg-primary/12 pointer-events-none absolute -top-20 -right-20 size-72 rounded-full blur-3xl"
          />
          <div
            aria-hidden
            className="bg-primary/8 pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-24 right-32 size-52 rounded-full blur-3xl"
          />
          <div className="relative">
            <div className="text-primary font-mono text-[10.5px] font-medium tracking-[0.18em] uppercase">
              {t("featuredLabel")}
            </div>
            <h1 className="serif mt-2.5 text-4xl leading-tight tracking-tight">
              <FeaturedTitle name={locale === "zh" && featured.nameZh ? featured.nameZh : featured.name} />
            </h1>
            {(featured.descriptionZh || featured.description) && (
              <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-2.5 max-w-md text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed">
                {locale === "zh" && featured.descriptionZh
                  ? featured.descriptionZh
                  : featured.description}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      <section className="mb-2 flex items-baseline justify-between">
        <h2 className="serif text-xl tracking-tight">{t("trendingTitle")}</h2>
```

> AGENT

Survey done. Two real targets:

1. **`ExtGrid` zero-results** — currently hardcoded English ("No results for ..."). Needs i18n + a "Clear filters" CTA when filters are active. Used on the listing AND home page.
2. **Publish dashboard empty** — bare text in a dashed box. Add icon + a "Create your first extension" CTA matching the new boundary-card pattern for visual consistency.

**Out of scope (deliberately):**
- Detail page's "no related / no tags" sections — currently hidden via conditional render. Hiding an empty card is actually the right call for cards-as-secondary-info; no work needed.
- Sidebar Installed/Saved counters hardcoded `0` — that's a wiring TODO (no `/installed` route exists), not an empty-state design issue.

Plan: i18n the ExtGrid strings, add a Clear filters button (only when filters active), match the dashed-card style; redo the Publish empty state to match. New i18n keys in both locales. ~3 files changed.

Sound good?

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/extension/ext-grid.tsx",
  "content": "import { SearchX } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { getTranslations } from \"next-intl/server\";\n\nimport type { ExtensionListItem } from \"@/lib/db/queries/extensions\";\nimport { Link } from \"@/lib/i18n/navigation\";\nimport type { Locale } from \"@/types\";\n\nimport { ExtCard } from \"./ext-card\";\n\ninterface ExtGridProps {\n  items: ExtensionListItem[];\n  locale?: Locale;\n  query?: string;\n  // When provided, the empty state shows a \"Clear filters\" link to this href.\n  // Pass for routes where filters are user-driven (the listing page); omit on\n  // routes where the filter set is baked in (e.g. the home Trending grid).\n  clearFiltersHref?: string;\n}\n\nexport async function ExtGrid({\n  items,\n  locale = \"en\",\n  query,\n  clearFiltersHref,\n}: ExtGridProps) {\n  if (items.length === 0) {\n    const t = await getTranslations(\"extensions.empty\");\n    const filtersActive = Boolean(query) || Boolean(clearFiltersHref);\n\n    return (\n      <div className=\"border-border bg-card/40 mx-auto my-2 flex max-w-md flex-col items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-dashed p-10 text-center\">\n        <SearchX className=\"text-muted-foreground/50 size-8\" />\n        <h2 className=\"text-foreground text-base font-semibold\">\n          {query\n            ? t(\"noResultsFor\", { query })\n            : filtersActive\n              ? t(\"noResultsFiltered\")\n              : t(\"noExtensionsYet\")}\n        </h2>\n        <p className=\"text-muted-foreground max-w-xs text-[13px] leading-relaxed\">\n          {filtersActive ? t(\"hintAdjust\") : t(\"hintCheckBack\")}\n        </p>\n        {clearFiltersHref && (\n          <Link\n            href={clearFiltersHref}\n            className=\"border-border text-foreground hover:border-primary/50 hover:text-primary mt-1 inline-flex items-center rounded-md border px-3.5 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors\"\n          >\n            {t(\"clearFilters\")}\n          </Link>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n  return (\n    <div className=\"grid grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(280px,1fr))] gap-4\">\n      {items.map((ext) => (\n        <ExtCard key={ext.id} ext={ext} locale={locale} />\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

**Files:**
- `components/extension/ext-grid.tsx` — i18n the empty state (now async server component to read translations); add an optional `clearFiltersHref` that, when passed, renders a "Clear filters" link; switch to dashed-card visual matching the new error boundaries; differentiate three states (search query / filtered with no query / never-any-extensions)
- `app/[locale]/extensions/page.tsx` — pass `clearFiltersHref="/extensions"` so the listing's empty state shows the CTA. Home keeps using ExtGrid without the prop, so its baked-in `dept=__all` filter won't surface a misleading "Clear filters"
- `app/[locale]/publish/page.tsx` — replace the bare-text empty state with the dashed-card pattern, add a `Package` icon, add a "Publish your first extension" CTA linking to `/publish/new`
- `lib/i18n/messages/{en,zh}.json` — `extensions.empty.*` (six keys) + `publish.dashboard.emptyTitle/emptyCta`

**Proposed commit:**

```
polish(empty-states): unified empty cards for listing and publish dashboard

- ExtGrid now reads strings via next-intl (was hardcoded English) and
  renders a dashed-card empty state matching the error boundaries.
  Differentiates three cases: search query with no hits, filters
  active with no hits, and "no extensions yet" for routes where the
  filter set is baked in (home Trending).
- A new optional clearFiltersHref renders a "Clear filters" link
  only when filters are user-driven. The listing passes it; the
  home Trending grid omits it so its dept=__all baked-in filter
  doesn't surface a misleading CTA.
- Publish dashboard's empty state is upgraded to the same dashed
  card with a Package icon and a "Publish your first extension" CTA
  that drops the user straight into /publish/new.
```

OK to commit, push, open PR?

- #15 — empty-state sweep

Empty-state sweep done. A11y audit is the remaining option from this session's plan — but as I flagged before, that wants its own dedicated session (axe will produce 20+ findings to triage; the call which to fix is yours, not mine).

Good place to stop?

> DEVELOPER

what will a11y audit do ?

> AGENT

Concretely, an a11y audit on this codebase would produce three things:

**1. Automated scan output** — read every interactive component (filter bar, dept popover, sidebar, install/save buttons, hero, top bar) and find:
- Missing `aria-label` on icon-only buttons
- Missing `alt` / `aria-hidden` on decorative SVGs
- Custom `role="button"` divs that should be real `<button>`s
- Form inputs without associated `<label>`
- Focus-visible rings that don't show or don't have enough contrast
- Custom popovers/menus that don't trap or restore focus

**2. Color-contrast check** — Editorial Ivory's `--muted-foreground` and `--primary` text need WCAG AA 4.5:1 against their backgrounds. Some of the small `[12.5px]` muted-foreground text used in cards and sidebars is the most likely failure.

**3. Keyboard-nav walk** — manually tab through each main route, note: focus order, traps (popovers stealing focus that won't escape), missing skip-link, custom widgets without arrow-key support. Many of these can't be tested in vitest — they need Playwright or hand-driving.

**The output:**
- One PR with unambiguous fixes (icon-button labels, semantic HTML, missing aria-hidden, focus-visible polish).
- A short memo / issue listing items that need your call — typically: contrast tweaks (changes the visual feel), kbd-nav decisions (do popovers close on Esc only, or also Tab away?), copy for screen readers.

**What I can't do in a Claude Code session:**
- Run axe-core programmatically without spinning up Playwright (slow, brittle in this env).
- Verify with a real screen reader.
- Test mobile-touch a11y.

So: I'd ship maybe 60-70% of the findings as code, and write up the rest for you to decide. Realistic scope: 1 PR + 1 memo issue.

Want me to start, or hold off?

35	    { key: "all", label: t("allExtensions"), Icon: Boxes },
36	    { key: "skills", label: t("skills"), Icon: Zap },
37	    { key: "mcp", label: t("mcpServers"), Icon: Globe2 },
38	    { key: "slash", label: t("slashCommands"), Icon: Command },
39	    { key: "plugins", label: t("plugins"), Icon: Plug },
40	  ];
41	
42	  function buildHref(updates: Record<string, string | null>): string {
43	    const params = new URLSearchParams(searchParams.toString());
44	    params.delete("page");
45	    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(updates)) {
46	      if (v === null) params.delete(k);
47	      else params.set(k, v);
48	    }
49	    const qs = params.toString();
50	    return `/extensions${qs ? `?${qs}` : ""}`;
51	  }
52	
53	  const activeCategory: ExtensionCategory | "all" =
54	    (searchParams.get("category") as ExtensionCategory | null) ?? "all";
55	  const activeFuncCat = searchParams.get("funcCat");
56	  const activeSubCat = searchParams.get("subCat");
57	  const activeL2 = searchParams.get("l2");
58	
59	  const [expandedSections, setExpandedSections] = useState({
60	    browse: true,
61	    categories: true,
62	    collections: false,
63	  });
64	  const [expandedFuncCats, setExpandedFuncCats] = useState<
65	    Record<string, boolean>
66	  >(() => ({
67	    [activeFuncCat ?? "workTask"]: true,
68	  }));
69	  const [expandedL1, setExpandedL1] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>(() =>
70	    activeSubCat ? { [activeSubCat]: true } : {},
71	  );
72	
73	  return (
74	    <aside
75	      className={cn(
76	        "bg-sidebar border-border flex flex-col overflow-hidden border-r transition-[width] duration-200 ease-in-out",
77	        collapsed ? "w-0" : "w-[240px]",
78	      )}
79	    >
80	      <div className="min-w-[200px] flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3">
81	        <SidebarSection
82	          title={t("browse")}
83	          expanded={expandedSections.browse}
84	          onToggle={() =>
85	            setExpandedSections((p) => ({ ...p, browse: !p.browse }))
86	          }
87	        >
88	          {BROWSE_ITEMS.map((item) => {
89	            const isActive = activeCategory === item.key;
90	            const next = isActive
91	              ? null
92	              : item.key === "all"
93	                ? null
94	                : item.key;
95	            const href = buildHref({ category: next });
96	            const count =
97	              item.key === "all"
98	                ? null
99	                : EXTENSIONS.filter((e) => e.category === item.key).length;
100	            return (
101	              <SidebarLink key={item.key} href={href} active={isActive}>
102	                <item.Icon className="size-[14px] shrink-0" />
103	                <span className="flex-1 truncate">{item.label}</span>
104	                {count !== null && (
105	                  <span className="text-[11px] opacity-55">{count}</span>
106	                )}
107	              </SidebarLink>
108	            );
109	          })}
110	        </SidebarSection>
111	
112	        <Divider />
113	
114	        <SidebarSection
115	          title={t("categories")}
116	          expanded={expandedSections.categories}
117	          onToggle={() =>
118	            setExpandedSections((p) => ({ ...p, categories: !p.categories }))
119	          }
120	        >
121	          <SidebarLink
122	            href={buildHref({ funcCat: null, subCat: null, l2: null })}
123	            active={!activeFuncCat}
124	          >
125	            <span className="bg-sidebar-foreground/40 size-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full" />
126	            <span className="flex-1">{t("all")}</span>
127	            <span className="text-[11px] opacity-55">{EXTENSIONS.length}</span>
128	          </SidebarLink>
129	
130	          {FUNC_TAXONOMY.map((cat) => {
131	            const isCatActive =
132	              activeFuncCat === cat.key && !activeSubCat && !activeL2;
133	            const isExpanded = expandedFuncCats[cat.key] ?? false;
134	            const catColor = FUNC_CAT_COLORS[cat.key];
135	            const catCount = EXTENSIONS.filter(
136	              (e) => e.funcCat === cat.key,
137	            ).length;
138	            const catHref = buildHref({
139	              funcCat: isCatActive ? null : cat.key,
140	              subCat: null,
141	              l2: null,
142	            });
143	            return (
144	              <div key={cat.key}>
145	                <div className="flex items-center">
146	                  <Link
147	                    href={catHref}
148	                    className={cn(
149	                      "flex flex-1 items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[13px] font-semibold transition",
150	                      isCatActive
151	                        ? "bg-sidebar-accent text-sidebar-accent-foreground"
152	                        : "text-sidebar-foreground hover:bg-sidebar-accent/50",
153	                    )}
154	                  >
155	                    <span
156	                      className="size-[7px] shrink-0 rounded-sm"
157	                      style={{ background: catColor }}
158	                    />
159	                    <span className="flex-1">{tx(`funcCat.${cat.key}`)}</span>
160	                    <span className="text-[11px] opacity-55">{catCount}</span>
161	                  </Link>
162	                  <button
163	                    type="button"
164	                    aria-label={isExpanded ? "Collapse" : "Expand"}
165	                    onClick={() =>
166	                      setExpandedFuncCats((p) => ({
167	                        ...p,
168	                        [cat.key]: !p[cat.key],
169	                      }))
170	                    }
171	                    className="text-sidebar-foreground/60 hover:text-sidebar-foreground rounded p-1 transition-colors"
172	                  >
173	                    {isExpanded ? (
174	                      <ChevronDown className="size-3" />
175	                    ) : (
176	                      <ChevronRight className="size-3" />
177	                    )}
178	                  </button>
179	                </div>
180	
181	                {isExpanded &&
182	                  cat.l1.map((l1) => {
183	                    const isL1Active =
184	                      activeSubCat === l1.key && !activeL2;
185	                    const isL1Expanded = expandedL1[l1.key] ?? false;
186	                    const l1Count = EXTENSIONS.filter(
187	                      (e) => e.subCat === l1.key,
188	                    ).length;
189	                    const l1Href = buildHref({
190	                      funcCat: cat.key,
191	                      subCat: isL1Active ? null : l1.key,
192	                      l2: null,
193	                    });
194	                    return (
195	                      <div key={l1.key} className="ml-2">
196	                        <div className="flex items-center">
197	                          <Link
198	                            href={l1Href}
199	                            className={cn(
200	                              "flex flex-1 items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[12.5px] font-medium transition",
201	                              isL1Active
202	                                ? "bg-sidebar-accent text-sidebar-accent-foreground"
203	                                : "text-sidebar-foreground hover:bg-sidebar-accent/50",
204	                            )}
205	                          >
206	                            <span
207	                              className="w-[1.5px] shrink-0 self-stretch rounded"
208	                              style={{
209	                                background: catColor,
210	                                opacity: isL1Active ? 1 : 0.35,
211	                              }}
212	                            />
213	                            <span className="flex-1">{tx(`l1.${l1.key}`)}</span>
214	                            <span className="text-[11px] opacity-50">
215	                              {l1Count}
216	                            </span>
217	                          </Link>
218	                          {l1.l2.length > 0 && (
219	                            <button
220	                              type="button"
221	                              aria-label={isL1Expanded ? "Collapse" : "Expand"}
222	                              onClick={() =>
223	                                setExpandedL1((p) => ({
224	                                  ...p,
225	                                  [l1.key]: !p[l1.key],
226	                                }))
227	                              }
228	                              className="text-sidebar-foreground/50 hover:text-sidebar-foreground rounded p-1 transition-colors"
229	                            >
230	                              {isL1Expanded ? (
231	                                <ChevronDown className="size-[11px]" />
232	                              ) : (
233	                                <ChevronRight className="size-[11px]" />
234	                              )}
235	                            </button>
236	                          )}
237	                        </div>
238	
239	                        {isL1Expanded &&
240	                          l1.l2.map((l2key) => {
241	                            const isL2Active = activeL2 === l2key;
242	                            const l2Href = buildHref({
243	                              funcCat: cat.key,
244	                              subCat: l1.key,
245	                              l2: isL2Active ? null : l2key,
246	                            });
247	                            return (
248	                              <Link
249	                                key={l2key}
250	                                href={l2Href}
251	                                className={cn(
252	                                  "flex w-full items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md py-1 pr-2 pl-6 text-left text-[12px] transition",
253	                                  isL2Active
254	                                    ? "bg-sidebar-accent text-sidebar-accent-foreground"
255	                                    : "text-sidebar-foreground/85 hover:bg-sidebar-accent/50",
256	                                )}
257	                              >
258	                                <span className="bg-sidebar-foreground/40 size-[3px] shrink-0 rounded-full" />
259	                                {tx(`l2.${l2key}`)}
260	                              </Link>
261	                            );
262	                          })}
263	                      </div>
264	                    );
265	                  })}
266	              </div>
267	            );
268	          })}
269	        </SidebarSection>
270	
271	        <Divider />
272	
273	        <SidebarSection
274	          title={t("collections")}
275	          expanded={expandedSections.collections}
276	          onToggle={() =>
277	            setExpandedSections((p) => ({
278	              ...p,
279	              collections: !p.collections,
280	            }))
281	          }
282	        >
283	          <SidebarStaticItem>
284	            <Folder className="size-[14px] shrink-0" />
285	            <span className="flex-1">{t("installed")}</span>
286	            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] opacity-60">0</span>
287	          </SidebarStaticItem>
288	          <SidebarStaticItem>
289	            <Folder className="size-[14px] shrink-0" />
290	            <span className="flex-1">{t("saved")}</span>
291	            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] opacity-60">0</span>
292	          </SidebarStaticItem>
293	          {COLLECTIONS.map((col) => (
294	            <SidebarStaticItem key={col.id}>
295	              <span className="bg-sidebar-primary size-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full" />
296	              <span className="flex-1 truncate">{col.name}</span>
297	              <span className="font-mono text-[11px] opacity-60">
298	                {col.count}
299	              </span>
300	            </SidebarStaticItem>
301	          ))}
302	          <SidebarStaticItem muted>
303	            <Plus className="size-[14px] shrink-0" />
304	            <span className="flex-1">{t("newGroup")}</span>
305	          </SidebarStaticItem>
306	        </SidebarSection>
307	      </div>
308	    </aside>
309	  );
310	}
311	
312	interface SidebarSectionProps {
313	  title: string;
314	  expanded: boolean;
315	  onToggle: () => void;
316	  children: React.ReactNode;
317	}
318	
319	function SidebarSection({
320	  title,
321	  expanded,
322	  onToggle,
323	  children,
324	}: SidebarSectionProps) {
325	  return (
326	    <div className="mb-1">
327	      <button
328	        type="button"
329	        onClick={onToggle}
330	        className="text-sidebar-foreground/85 hover:text-sidebar-foreground flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase transition-colors"
331	      >
332	        {title}
333	        {expanded ? (
334	          <ChevronDown className="size-[14px]" />
335	        ) : (
336	          <ChevronRight className="size-[14px]" />
337	        )}
338	      </button>
339	      {expanded && <div className="mt-0.5 flex flex-col gap-px">{children}</div>}
340	    </div>
341	  );
342	}
343	
344	function SidebarLink({
345	  href,
346	  active,
347	  children,
348	}: {
349	  href: string;
350	  active?: boolean;
351	  children: React.ReactNode;
352	}) {
353	  return (
354	    <Link
355	      href={href}
356	      scroll={false}
357	      className={cn(
358	        "flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[13px] font-medium transition",
359	        active
360	          ? "bg-sidebar-accent text-sidebar-accent-foreground font-semibold"
361	          : "text-sidebar-foreground hover:bg-sidebar-accent/50",
362	      )}
363	    >
364	      {children}
365	    </Link>
366	  );
367	}
368	
369	function SidebarStaticItem({
370	  muted,
371	  children,
372	}: {
373	  muted?: boolean;
374	  children: React.ReactNode;
375	}) {
376	  return (
377	    <button
378	      type="button"
379	      className={cn(
380	        "text-sidebar-foreground hover:bg-sidebar-accent/50 flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[13px] font-medium transition",
381	        muted && "opacity-60",
382	      )}
383	    >
384	      {children}
385	    </button>
386	  );
387	}
388	
389	function Divider() {
390	  return <div className="bg-sidebar-border mx-1 my-3 h-px" />;
391	}

1	"use client";
2	
3	import { LogOut } from "lucide-react";
4	import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
5	import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
6	
7	import { authClient } from "@/lib/auth/client";
8	import { Link, useRouter } from "@/lib/i18n/navigation";
9	
10	export function UserButton() {
11	  const t = useTranslations("auth.userMenu");
12	  const router = useRouter();
13	  // useSession is reactive — it re-renders when sign-in/sign-out happens
14	  // via the same authClient, so the avatar appears without a page refresh.
15	  const { data } = authClient.useSession();
16	  const session = data ? { user: data.user } : null;
17	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
18	  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
19	
20	  useEffect(() => {
21	    function onClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
22	      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
23	        setOpen(false);
24	      }
25	    }
26	    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onClickOutside);
27	    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onClickOutside);
28	  }, []);
29	
30	  if (!session) {
31	    return (
32	      <Link
33	        href="/sign-in"
34	        className="bg-secondary border-border text-foreground hover:bg-secondary/70 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-semibold transition-colors"
35	      >
36	        {t("signIn")}
37	      </Link>
38	    );
39	  }
40	
41	  async function handleSignOut() {
42	    try {
43	      await authClient.signOut();
44	      router.push("/");
45	      router.refresh();
46	    } catch (err) {
47	      console.error("Sign-out failed:", err);
48	    } finally {
49	      setOpen(false);
50	    }
51	  }
52	
53	  const initials = session.user.name
54	    ? session.user.name
55	        .split(" ")
56	        .map((w) => w[0])
57	        .join("")
58	        .slice(0, 2)
59	        .toUpperCase()
60	    : session.user.email[0].toUpperCase();
61	
62	  return (
63	    <div ref={ref} className="relative">
64	      <button
65	        type="button"
66	        onClick={() => setOpen((p) => !p)}
67	        className="bg-primary/10 border-primary/40 flex size-8 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center rounded-full border-[1.5px] text-[11px] font-bold transition hover:bg-primary/20"
68	      >
69	        <span className="text-primary">{initials}</span>
70	      </button>
71	
72	      {open && (
73	        <div className="bg-card border-border absolute right-0 top-10 z-50 min-w-[160px] rounded-lg border py-1 shadow-lg">
74	          <div className="border-border border-b px-3 py-2.5">
75	            <p className="text-foreground truncate text-[12px] font-semibold">
76	              {session.user.name ?? session.user.email}
77	            </p>
78	            <p className="text-muted-foreground truncate text-[11px]">
79	              {session.user.email}
80	            </p>
81	          </div>
82	          <button
83	            type="button"
84	            onClick={handleSignOut}
85	            className="text-foreground hover:bg-secondary flex w-full items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-left text-[12px] transition"
86	          >
87	            <LogOut className="size-3.5" />
88	            {t("signOut")}
89	          </button>
90	        </div>
91	      )}
92	    </div>
93	  );
94	}
95

1	"use client";
2	
3	import { Moon, Sun } from "lucide-react";
4	import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
5	import { useTransition } from "react";
6	
7	import { THEME_COOKIE_NAME, type Theme } from "@/lib/theme";
8	
9	interface ThemeSwitchProps {
10	  theme: Theme;
11	}
12	
13	export function ThemeSwitch({ theme }: ThemeSwitchProps) {
14	  const router = useRouter();
15	  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();
16	
17	  function toggle() {
18	    const next: Theme = theme === "ivory" ? "dark" : "ivory";
19	    document.cookie = `${THEME_COOKIE_NAME}=${next}; path=/; max-age=${60 * 60 * 24 * 365}; samesite=lax`;
20	    startTransition(() => {
21	      router.refresh();
22	    });
23	  }
24	
25	  return (
26	    <button
27	      type="button"
28	      onClick={toggle}
29	      disabled={pending}
30	      aria-label={
31	        theme === "ivory" ? "Switch to dark theme" : "Switch to ivory theme"
32	      }
33	      className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-secondary rounded-md p-1.5 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
34	    >
35	      {theme === "ivory" ? (
36	        <Moon className="size-[16px]" />
37	      ) : (
38	        <Sun className="size-[16px]" />
39	      )}
40	    </button>
41	  );
42	}
43

1	"use client";
2	
3	import { Globe } from "lucide-react";
4	import { useTransition } from "react";
5	
6	import { usePathname, useRouter } from "@/lib/i18n/navigation";
7	import type { Locale } from "@/types";
8	
9	export function LocaleSwitch({ locale }: { locale: Locale }) {
10	  const router = useRouter();
11	  const pathname = usePathname();
12	  const [, startTransition] = useTransition();
13	
14	  const nextLocale: Locale = locale === "en" ? "zh" : "en";
15	  const label = locale === "en" ? "中文" : "EN";
16	
17	  function handleSwitch() {
18	    startTransition(() => {
19	      router.replace(pathname, { locale: nextLocale });
20	    });
21	  }
22	
23	  return (
24	    <button
25	      type="button"
26	      onClick={handleSwitch}
27	      className="bg-secondary border-border text-foreground hover:bg-secondary/70 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-semibold transition-colors"
28	    >
29	      <Globe className="text-muted-foreground size-[13px]" />
30	      {label}
31	    </button>
32	  );
33	}
34

15	} from "@/lib/data/departments";
16	import { useFilters } from "@/lib/hooks/use-filters";
17	import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
18	import type { Department } from "@/types";
19	
20	const ALL_DEPTS_TOKEN = "__all";
21	
22	export function DeptPicker() {
23	  const { filters, update } = useFilters();
24	  // Default = MY_DEPT_ID per the prototype's behavior; explicit "__all"
25	  // disables the filter.
26	  const active = filters.dept ?? MY_DEPT_ID;
27	  const isAll = active === ALL_DEPTS_TOKEN;
28	  const isMine = active === MY_DEPT_ID;
29	  const path = isAll ? null : deptPath(active, "en");
30	
31	  function pick(id: string) {
32	    if (id === MY_DEPT_ID) {
33	      update({ dept: undefined }); // undefined falls back to default = MY_DEPT_ID
34	    } else {
35	      update({ dept: id });
36	    }
37	  }
38	
39	  const trigger = (
40	    <button
41	      type="button"
42	      className="bg-card border-border hover:border-primary/40 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] outline-none transition data-[popup-open]:border-primary"
43	    >
44	      <Building2 className="text-muted-foreground size-3.5" />
45	      <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5">
46	        {isMine && (
47	          <span className="bg-primary size-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full" />
48	        )}
49	        {isAll ? (
50	          <span className="font-semibold">All Departments</span>
51	        ) : (
52	          path && (
53	            <>
54	              {path.length > 1 && (
55	                <span className="text-muted-foreground truncate text-[11.5px]">
56	                  {path.slice(0, -1).join(" / ")} /
57	                </span>
58	              )}
59	              <span className="font-semibold">{path[path.length - 1]}</span>
60	            </>
61	          )
62	        )}
63	      </span>
64	      <ChevronDown className="text-muted-foreground size-3.5" />
65	    </button>
66	  );
67	
68	  return (
69	    <Popover>
70	      <PopoverTrigger render={trigger} />
71	      <PopoverContent
72	        className="w-[360px] p-0"
73	        align="start"
74	        sideOffset={6}
75	      >
76	        <div className="border-border flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 border-b p-2.5">
77	          <Chip active={isMine} onClick={() => pick(MY_DEPT_ID)}>
78	            <span
79	              className={cn(
80	                "size-1.5 rounded-full",
81	                isMine ? "bg-primary-foreground" : "bg-primary",
82	              )}
83	            />
84	            My Department
85	          </Chip>
86	          <Chip active={active === "eng"} onClick={() => pick("eng")}>
87	            My Org
88	          </Chip>
89	          <Chip active={isAll} onClick={() => pick(ALL_DEPTS_TOKEN)}>
90	            All Departments
91	          </Chip>
92	        </div>
93	        <div className="max-h-72 overflow-y-auto py-1">
94	          <DeptTree
95	            list={DEPARTMENTS}
96	            depth={0}
97	            active={active}
98	            onPick={pick}
99	          />
100	        </div>
101	      </PopoverContent>
102	    </Popover>
103	  );
104	}
105	
106	function Chip({
107	  active,
108	  onClick,
109	  children,
110	}: {
111	  active: boolean;
112	  onClick: () => void;
113	  children: React.ReactNode;
114	}) {
115	  return (
116	    <button
117	      type="button"
118	      onClick={onClick}
119	      className={cn(
120	        "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[11.5px] font-semibold transition",
121	        active
122	          ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground"
123	          : "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-accent",
124	      )}
125	    >
126	      {children}
127	    </button>
128	  );
129	}
130	
131	interface DeptTreeProps {
132	  list: Department[];
133	  depth: number;
134	  active: string;
135	  onPick: (id: string) => void;
136	}
137	
138	function DeptTree({ list, depth, active, onPick }: DeptTreeProps) {
139	  return (
140	    <div>
141	      {list.map((d) => (
142	        <DeptNode
143	          key={d.id}
144	          dept={d}
145	          depth={depth}
146	          active={active}
147	          onPick={onPick}
148	        />
149	      ))}
150	    </div>
151	  );
152	}
153	
154	function DeptNode({
155	  dept,
156	  depth,
157	  active,
158	  onPick,
159	}: {
160	  dept: Department;
161	  depth: number;
162	  active: string;
163	  onPick: (id: string) => void;
164	}) {
165	  // Auto-expand the path leading to MY_DEPT_ID.
166	  const initial =
167	    MY_DEPT_ID === dept.id || MY_DEPT_ID.startsWith(`${dept.id}.`);
168	  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(initial);
169	  const isActive = active === dept.id;
170	  const indent = 8 + depth * 14;
171	
172	  return (
173	    <>
174	      <div
175	        className="flex items-center"
176	        style={{ paddingLeft: indent }}
177	      >
178	        {dept.children ? (
179	          <button
180	            type="button"
181	            aria-label={expanded ? "Collapse" : "Expand"}
182	            onClick={(e) => {
183	              e.stopPropagation();
184	              setExpanded((p) => !p);
185	            }}
186	            className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground flex size-5 items-center justify-center rounded transition-colors"
187	          >
188	            {expanded ? (
189	              <ChevronDown className="size-3" />
190	            ) : (
191	              <ChevronRight className="size-3" />
192	            )}
193	          </button>
194	        ) : (
195	          <span className="size-5 shrink-0" />
196	        )}
197	        <button
198	          type="button"
199	          onClick={() => onPick(dept.id)}
200	          className={cn(
201	            "mx-1 flex flex-1 items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-left text-[12.5px] transition",
202	            isActive
203	              ? "bg-primary/10 text-primary font-semibold"
204	              : "text-foreground hover:bg-accent",
205	          )}
206	        >
207	          <span className="flex-1">{dept.name}</span>
208	          {dept.id === MY_DEPT_ID && (
209	            <span className="bg-primary/10 text-primary rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9.5px] font-bold tracking-wider">
210	              MINE
211	            </span>
212	          )}
213	        </button>
214	      </div>
215	      {dept.children && expanded && (
216	        <DeptTree
217	          list={dept.children}
218	          depth={depth + 1}
219	          active={active}
220	          onPick={onPick}
221	        />
222	      )}
223	    </>
224	  );
225	}
226

1	"use client";
2	
3	import { ChevronDown, X } from "lucide-react";
4	import { useState } from "react";
5	
6	import type { TagWithCount } from "@/lib/db/queries/tags";
7	import { useFilters } from "@/lib/hooks/use-filters";
8	import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
9	
10	interface TagDrawerProps {
11	  tags: TagWithCount[];
12	}
13	
14	const VISIBLE_DEFAULT = 14;
15	
16	export function TagDrawer({ tags }: TagDrawerProps) {
17	  const { filters, update } = useFilters();
18	  const active = filters.tags ?? [];
19	  const tagMatch = filters.tagMatch ?? "any";
20	  const initiallyOpen = active.length > 0;
21	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(initiallyOpen);
22	  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false);
23	
24	  function toggle(id: string) {
25	    update({
26	      tags: active.includes(id) ? active.filter((t) => t !== id) : [...active, id],
27	    });
28	  }
29	
30	  const visible = showAll ? tags : tags.slice(0, VISIBLE_DEFAULT);
31	  const hiddenCount = Math.max(0, tags.length - VISIBLE_DEFAULT);
32	
33	  return (
34	    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
35	      <button
36	        type="button"
37	        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
38	        className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] font-semibold transition-colors"
39	      >
40	        <ChevronDown
41	          className={cn(
42	            "size-3.5 transition-transform",
43	            open ? "" : "-rotate-90",
44	          )}
45	        />
46	        # Tags
47	        {active.length > 0 && (
48	          <span className="bg-primary text-primary-foreground rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold">
49	            {active.length}
50	          </span>
51	        )}
52	      </button>
53	
54	      {open && (
55	        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 pt-1">
56	          {visible.map((t) => {
57	            const isActive = active.includes(t.id);
58	            return (
59	              <button
60	                key={t.id}
61	                type="button"
62	                onClick={() => toggle(t.id)}
63	                className={cn(
64	                  "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold transition",
65	                  isActive
66	                    ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground border-primary"
67	                    : "border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary hover:text-primary",
68	                )}
69	              >
70	                {t.id}
71	                <span
72	                  className={cn(
73	                    "text-[10px]",
74	                    isActive ? "opacity-70" : "opacity-50",
75	                  )}
76	                >
77	                  {t.count}
78	                </span>
79	              </button>
80	            );
81	          })}
82	
83	          {!showAll && hiddenCount > 0 && (
84	            <button
85	              type="button"
86	              onClick={() => setShowAll(true)}
87	              className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground text-[11.5px] font-semibold underline underline-offset-2 transition-colors"
88	            >
89	              +{hiddenCount} more
90	            </button>
91	          )}
92	          {showAll && hiddenCount > 0 && (
93	            <button
94	              type="button"
95	              onClick={() => setShowAll(false)}
96	              className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground text-[11.5px] font-semibold underline underline-offset-2 transition-colors"
97	            >
98	              − less
99	            </button>
100	          )}
101	
102	          {active.length > 0 && (
103	            <>
104	              <div className="bg-muted ml-2 flex rounded p-0.5">
105	                {(["any", "all"] as const).map((m) => (
106	                  <button
107	                    key={m}
108	                    type="button"
109	                    onClick={() =>
110	                      update({ tagMatch: m === "any" ? undefined : m })
111	                    }
112	                    className={cn(
113	                      "rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase transition",
114	                      tagMatch === m
115	                        ? "bg-card text-foreground shadow-sm"
116	                        : "text-muted-foreground",
117	                    )}
118	                  >
119	                    {m}
120	                  </button>
121	                ))}
122	              </div>
123	              <button
124	                type="button"
125	                onClick={() => update({ tags: undefined })}
126	                className="border-border text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold transition"
127	              >
128	                <X className="size-3" />
129	                Clear tags
130	              </button>
131	            </>
132	          )}
133	        </div>
134	      )}
135	    </div>
136	  );
137	}
138

1	"use client";
2	
3	import { Check, Download } from "lucide-react";
4	import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
5	import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
6	
7	import { installExtension } from "@/lib/actions/install";
8	import { useRouter } from "@/lib/i18n/navigation";
9	
10	type Size = "sm" | "lg";
11	
12	interface InstallButtonProps {
13	  extensionId: string;
14	  size?: Size;
15	}
16	
17	export function InstallButton({
18	  extensionId,
19	  size = "sm",
20	}: InstallButtonProps) {
21	  const t = useTranslations("common");
22	  const router = useRouter();
23	  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();
24	  const [installed, setInstalled] = useState(false);
25	
26	  async function handleClick() {
27	    startTransition(async () => {
28	      const result = await installExtension(extensionId);
29	
30	      if (!result.ok && result.error === "unauthenticated") {
31	        router.push("/sign-in");
32	        return;
33	      }
34	
35	      if (result.ok) {
36	        setInstalled(true);
37	        // Reset after 3 s so the user can see the confirmation.
38	        setTimeout(() => setInstalled(false), 3000);
39	      }
40	    });
41	  }
42	
43	  if (size === "lg") {
44	    return (
45	      <button
46	        type="button"
47	        onClick={handleClick}
48	        disabled={isPending || installed}
49	        className="bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:opacity-90 inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-bold transition disabled:opacity-70"
50	      >
51	        {installed ? (
52	          <Check className="size-3.5" />
53	        ) : (
54	          <Download className="size-3.5" />
55	        )}
56	        {isPending
57	          ? t("installing")
58	          : installed
59	            ? t("installed")
60	            : t("install")}
61	      </button>
62	    );
63	  }
64	
65	  return (
66	    <button
67	      type="button"
68	      onClick={handleClick}
69	      disabled={isPending || installed}
70	      className="bg-primary text-primary-foreground flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-bold transition hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-70"
71	    >
72	      {installed ? (
73	        <Check className="size-3" />
74	      ) : (
75	        <Download className="size-3" />
76	      )}
77	      {isPending
78	        ? t("installing")
79	        : installed
80	          ? t("installed")
81	          : t("install")}
82	    </button>
83	  );
84	}
85

16	});

29	  display: "swap",
30	});

32	export default async function LocaleLayout({
33	  children,
34	  params,
35	}: {
36	  children: React.ReactNode;
37	  params: Promise<{ locale: string }>;
38	}) {

43	  return (
44	    <html
45	      lang={locale}
46	      className={`${inter.variable} ${fraunces.variable} ${jetbrainsMono.variable} h-full antialiased ${theme === "dark" ? "dark" : ""}`}
47	    >
48	      <body className="bg-background text-foreground h-full">
49	        <NextIntlClientProvider messages={messages}>
50	          <AppShell theme={theme} locale={locale as Locale}>
51	            {children}
52	          </AppShell>
53	        </NextIntlClientProvider>
54	      </body>
55	    </html>

13	  theme: Theme;
14	  locale: Locale;

17	export function AppShell({ children, theme, locale }: AppShellProps) {
18	  const [sidebarCollapsed, setSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);
19	
20	  return (
21	    <div className="flex h-screen flex-col overflow-hidden">
22	      <TopBar
23	        theme={theme}
24	        locale={locale}
25	        onToggleSidebar={() => setSidebarCollapsed((p) => !p)}
26	      />
27	      <div className="flex flex-1 overflow-hidden">
28	        <Sidebar collapsed={sidebarCollapsed} />
29	        <main className="bg-background flex-1 overflow-auto">{children}</main>
30	      </div>
31	    </div>
32	  );
33	}
34

> AGENT

Plan based on the survey. Here's what I'll fix in this PR:

**Tier 1 — real a11y bugs:**
- Top-bar search input has no label (SR hears "edit" with no context). Add `role="search"` to form + i18n'd `aria-label` on input.
- Filter components have hardcoded English labels (Trending / Personal / Sort by) — broken for zh users. Move to existing `filters.*` translation keys.
- Sidebar's `Installed` / `Saved` items are focusable buttons that do nothing — keyboard users tab through dead buttons. Convert to non-interactive items with a "soon" badge.
- Skip-to-content link missing — keyboard users can't bypass topbar/sidebar. Add to app shell.
- Top-bar `docs` link goes to `#` — mark `aria-disabled` + remove from tab order.

**Tier 2 — polish:**
- `aria-expanded` on all collapsible section toggles (sidebar headers, tag drawer, dept tree chevrons).
- `aria-pressed` on toggle chips (filter chips, scope pills, tag chips).
- `aria-label` i18n on icon-only buttons (theme switch, locale switch, sidebar chevrons).
- `role="group"` + `aria-label` on chip groups.

**Memo (separate file, not code):**
- UserButton popover → migrate to Base UI Popover/Menu (focus trap, Esc, role=menu).
- Color contrast audit on `text-muted-foreground` against ivory backgrounds.
- Global `focus-visible` ring style — currently inconsistent.
- Phase-9 follow-ups (Installed/Saved wiring, docs link destination).

Tier 1+2 hits ~10 files. Memo is one new doc. Let me do it.

  "old_string": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useFilters } from \"@/lib/hooks/use-filters\";\nimport type { Filters } from \"@/lib/validators/filters\";\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\n\nconst CHIPS = [\n  { key: \"all\", label: \"All\" },\n  { key: \"trending\", label: \"Trending\" },\n  { key: \"new\", label: \"New\" },\n  { key: \"official\", label: \"Official\" },\n  { key: \"free\", label: \"Open Source\" },\n] as const;\n\nexport function FilterChips() {\n  const { filters, update } = useFilters();\n  const active = filters.filter ?? \"all\";\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1.5\">\n      {CHIPS.map((c) => {\n        const isActive = active === c.key;\n        return (\n          <button\n            key={c.key}\n            type=\"button\"\n            onClick={() =>\n              update({\n                filter: c.key === \"all\" ? undefined : (c.key as Filters[\"filter\"]),\n              })\n            }\n            className={cn(\n              \"rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-semibold transition\",\n              isActive\n                ? \"bg-foreground text-background border-foreground\"\n                : \"border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary hover:text-primary\",\n            )}\n          >\n            {c.label}\n          </button>\n        );\n      })}\n    </div>\n  );\n}",
  "new_string": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\n\nimport { useFilters } from \"@/lib/hooks/use-filters\";\nimport type { Filters } from \"@/lib/validators/filters\";\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\n\nconst CHIP_KEYS = [\"all\", \"trending\", \"new\", \"official\", \"free\"] as const;\n\nexport function FilterChips() {\n  const t = useTranslations(\"filters\");\n  const { filters, update } = useFilters();\n  const active = filters.filter ?? \"all\";\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      role=\"group\"\n      aria-label={t(\"filtersLabel\")}\n      className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1.5\"\n    >\n      {CHIP_KEYS.map((key) => {\n        const isActive = active === key;\n        return (\n          <button\n            key={key}\n            type=\"button\"\n            aria-pressed={isActive}\n            onClick={() =>\n              update({\n                filter: key === \"all\" ? undefined : (key as Filters[\"filter\"]),\n              })\n            }\n            className={cn(\n              \"rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-semibold transition\",\n              isActive\n                ? \"bg-foreground text-background border-foreground\"\n                : \"border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary hover:text-primary\",\n            )}\n          >\n            {t(`chips.${key}`)}\n          </button>\n        );\n      })}\n    </div>\n  );\n}"
}

  "old_string": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { Globe } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useTransition } from \"react\";\n\nimport { usePathname, useRouter } from \"@/lib/i18n/navigation\";\nimport type { Locale } from \"@/types\";\n\nexport function LocaleSwitch({ locale }: { locale: Locale }) {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const pathname = usePathname();\n  const [, startTransition] = useTransition();\n\n  const nextLocale: Locale = locale === \"en\" ? \"zh\" : \"en\";\n  const label = locale === \"en\" ? \"中文\" : \"EN\";\n\n  function handleSwitch() {\n    startTransition(() => {\n      router.replace(pathname, { locale: nextLocale });\n    });\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <button\n      type=\"button\"\n      onClick={handleSwitch}\n      className=\"bg-secondary border-border text-foreground hover:bg-secondary/70 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-semibold transition-colors\"\n    >\n      <Globe className=\"text-muted-foreground size-[13px]\" />\n      {label}\n    </button>\n  );\n}",
  "new_string": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { Globe } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\nimport { useTransition } from \"react\";\n\nimport { usePathname, useRouter } from \"@/lib/i18n/navigation\";\nimport type { Locale } from \"@/types\";\n\nexport function LocaleSwitch({ locale }: { locale: Locale }) {\n  const t = useTranslations(\"nav\");\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const pathname = usePathname();\n  const [, startTransition] = useTransition();\n\n  const nextLocale: Locale = locale === \"en\" ? \"zh\" : \"en\";\n  const label = locale === \"en\" ? \"中文\" : \"EN\";\n  const ariaLabel = locale === \"en\" ? t(\"switchToZh\") : t(\"switchToEn\");\n\n  function handleSwitch() {\n    startTransition(() => {\n      router.replace(pathname, { locale: nextLocale });\n    });\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <button\n      type=\"button\"\n      onClick={handleSwitch}\n      aria-label={ariaLabel}\n      className=\"bg-secondary border-border text-foreground hover:bg-secondary/70 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-semibold transition-colors\"\n    >\n      <Globe aria-hidden className=\"text-muted-foreground size-[13px]\" />\n      {label}\n    </button>\n  );\n}"
}

  "new_string": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\n\nimport type { Theme } from \"@/lib/theme\";\nimport type { Locale } from \"@/types\";\n\nimport { Sidebar } from \"./sidebar\";\nimport { TopBar } from \"./top-bar\";\n\ninterface AppShellProps {\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n  theme: Theme;\n  locale: Locale;\n}\n\nexport function AppShell({ children, theme, locale }: AppShellProps) {\n  const t = useTranslations(\"nav\");\n  const [sidebarCollapsed, setSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex h-screen flex-col overflow-hidden\">\n      {/* Skip link — visually hidden until focused so keyboard users can\n          jump past the topbar + sidebar nav directly to the page content. */}\n      <a\n        href=\"#main-content\"\n        className=\"bg-primary text-primary-foreground focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-background sr-only z-50 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold focus-visible:not-sr-only focus-visible:fixed focus-visible:top-2 focus-visible:left-2 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-2\"\n      >\n        {t(\"skipToContent\")}\n      </a>\n      <TopBar\n        theme={theme}\n        locale={locale}\n        onToggleSidebar={() => setSidebarCollapsed((p) => !p)}\n      />\n      <div className=\"flex flex-1 overflow-hidden\">\n        <Sidebar collapsed={sidebarCollapsed} />\n        <main\n          id=\"main-content\"\n          tabIndex={-1}\n          className=\"bg-background flex-1 overflow-auto\"\n        >\n          {children}\n        </main>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/docs/a11y-memo.md",
  "content": "# Accessibility memo — items needing design input\n\nCompanion to the static a11y sweep (PR #16). The fixes that didn't need design\ninput are already applied: i18n on hardcoded ARIA labels and filter copy,\n`aria-expanded` / `aria-pressed` / `role=\"group\"` across toggleable widgets,\n`role=\"search\"` and an `aria-label` on the topbar search input, a skip-to-content\nlink, and converting the sidebar's \"Installed / Saved\" rows from focusable\nbuttons-that-do-nothing into non-interactive placeholders.\n\nWhat follows is the punch-list that needs a product or design call before code\nmoves.\n\n## 1. UserButton popover → Base UI Menu primitive\n\n`components/layout/user-button.tsx` is a hand-rolled popover (click-outside via\n`mousedown`, no `Esc` key handler, no focus trap, no focus restore on close, no\n`role=\"menu\"` / `role=\"menuitem\"` semantics).\n\nA11y fix: migrate to `@base-ui/react/menu` (already used elsewhere — see\n`components/ui/select.tsx` for the wrapper pattern). Base UI gives us:\n\n- `aria-haspopup=\"menu\"` on the trigger\n- `Esc` to close, focus restored to the trigger\n- Arrow-key navigation through items\n- `role=\"menu\"` / `role=\"menuitem\"` on the popup\n\nThis is a straight swap, ~30 LOC. Open question for the user: do we also want a\n`Settings` link in the menu? Currently the only item is `Sign out`, and the\nsign-in case is a separate element altogether.\n\n## 2. Color contrast on `text-muted-foreground`\n\n`oklch(48% 0.015 60)` against the ivory `--background` of `oklch(98%)` lands\naround **3.5 : 1** by my rough check. WCAG AA requires **4.5 : 1** for body text\nand **3 : 1** for UI/large. We use `text-muted-foreground` on plenty of small\nbody text (sidebar counts at `[11px]`, card descriptions at `[12.5px]`,\nmetadata rows in the About card, hint copy in empty states).\n\nLikely failing surfaces:\n\n- Sidebar function-cat / L1 / L2 counts (`[11px] opacity-55`)\n- Card descriptions and tag chips (`[12.5px] / [11px]`)\n- Detail page About card values (`[11.5px]`)\n- Empty-state hint paragraph (`[13px]`)\n\nTwo options:\n\n- **A — Darken the muted foreground** to ~`oklch(40% ...)` so all current\n  usages clear AA. Will affect the \"soft / editorial\" feel — probably a small\n  shift.\n- **B — Reserve `text-muted-foreground` for ≥ 14 px text** and define a\n  separate token for small captions that's darker. More plumbing.\n\nNeeds a designer eye.\n\n## 3. Global `focus-visible` ring\n\nToday the focus state is inconsistent: some buttons use the default browser\noutline, some declare `outline-none` with no replacement, some declare\n`focus-visible:ring-ring/20 focus-visible:ring-3`. Easy keyboard users to lose.\n\nSuggested: a single rule in `app/globals.css` like\n\n```css\n:where(button, a, input, select, textarea, [role=\"button\"]):focus-visible {\n  outline: 2px solid var(--ring);\n  outline-offset: 2px;\n}\n```\n\n…and audit individual `outline-none` declarations to see which still need a\ncustom override. Probably half a day's work but the visual fingerprint is\nproject-wide so it warrants a design call.\n\n## 4. Phase-9 follow-ups parked here\n\n- **Sidebar \"Installed\" / \"Saved\"** rows are now non-interactive placeholders\n  with hardcoded `0` counts. When per-user collections wire up (Phase 9 was\n  CLI v1; collection routes are post-v1), make them real links and wire counts\n  through. Until then the placeholder reads the same as a disabled link to AT.\n- **Top-bar `Docs` link** is a non-interactive placeholder (was `href=\"#\"`,\n  which leaked into tab order and went nowhere). Decide where docs live — an\n  external URL, an MDX route, or a redirect to the existing `docs/` md files\n  rendered server-side — then re-enable the link.\n\n## 5. Mobile a11y not covered\n\nTouch targets, swipe gestures, screen-reader pass on iOS / Android — out of\nscope for this static sweep. Worth a separate session with a real device once\nthe layout settles.\n"
}
```

**Files changed:** 14 modified, 1 new (`docs/a11y-memo.md`). All five tasks done.

**Proposed commit:**

```
polish(a11y): static a11y sweep across topbar, sidebar, and filters

The unambiguous fixes from the audit. Items needing design input
(UserButton menu refactor, contrast tokens, focus-visible style,
phase-9 wiring follow-ups) are written up in docs/a11y-memo.md.

i18n of hardcoded labels — these were both an a11y and an i18n bug
(zh users were hearing English):
- filter-chips, scope-pills, sort-select, tag-drawer, dept-picker:
  visible labels and chevron aria-labels via filters.* keys
- top-bar (toggle button, search input, docs placeholder), theme
  switch, locale switch, sidebar chevron buttons: aria-labels and
  visible captions via nav.* / filters.dept.* keys

ARIA wiring on toggleable widgets:
- aria-expanded on sidebar section toggles, tag-drawer trigger,
  dept-tree chevrons, user-menu trigger
- aria-pressed on filter chips, scope pills, tag chips, dept
  selection chips
- role="group" + aria-label on chip groups (filters / scope /
  match mode / tag panel)
- role="search" + aria-label on the topbar search form/input
- aria-haspopup="menu" on user-menu trigger
- aria-hidden on decorative icons that sit next to a visible label

Skip-to-content link in the app shell jumps focus past the topbar
and sidebar to a tabIndex=-1 main landmark; visually hidden until
focused.

Sidebar Installed/Saved/collection rows were focusable buttons
that did nothing — converted to non-interactive divs with
aria-disabled so keyboard users no longer tab through dead
controls. Topbar Docs link went to "#"; replaced with a non-
interactive placeholder until the docs route exists.

UserButton tests updated to assert aria-label / haspopup /
expanded; the visible initials are still asserted as text content
(now aria-hidden, since the accessible name comes from aria-label).
```

OK to commit, push, open PR?

{
  "command": "gh pr create --title \"polish(a11y): static a11y sweep across topbar, sidebar, and filters\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\nStatic a11y audit across the marketplace shell. Lands the unambiguous fixes that don't need design input. Items that do need a design call (UserButton menu refactor, contrast tokens, focus-visible style, phase-9 wiring follow-ups) are written up in **\\`docs/a11y-memo.md\\`**.\n\n### Real bugs fixed\n\n- **Search input had no label.** Topbar form gets \\`role=\"search\"\\`, the input gets an \\`aria-label\\` via \\`nav.searchLabel\\`. Screen-reader users no longer hear \"edit\" with no context.\n- **Filter components were hardcoded English** (Trending / Personal / Sort by / +N more / Clear tags / All Departments / My Department / My Org). Both an a11y and an i18n bug — zh users were hearing English. All moved to \\`filters.*\\` translation keys.\n- **Sidebar Installed/Saved/collection rows were focusable buttons that did nothing** — keyboard users tabbed through dead controls. Now non-interactive \\`<div aria-disabled>\\`.\n- **Topbar Docs link went to \\`href=\"#\"\\`** and leaked into the tab order. Replaced with a non-interactive placeholder until the docs route exists.\n- **No skip-to-content link.** Added one in the app shell that jumps focus past the topbar/sidebar to a \\`tabIndex=-1\\` main landmark; visually hidden until focused.\n\n### ARIA wiring polish\n\n- \\`aria-expanded\\` on every collapsible toggle (sidebar section headers, tag drawer, dept-tree chevrons, user-menu trigger).\n- \\`aria-pressed\\` on every toggle chip (filter chips, scope pills, tag chips, dept selection chips).\n- \\`role=\"group\"\\` + \\`aria-label\\`/\\`aria-labelledby\\` on chip groups (filters / scope / tag match mode / tag panel).\n- \\`aria-haspopup=\"menu\"\\` on the user-menu trigger.\n- \\`aria-hidden\\` on decorative icons that sit next to a visible text label.\n- i18n'd \\`aria-label\\` on icon-only buttons (toggle sidebar, theme switch, locale switch, sidebar chevrons, dept-tree chevrons).\n\n### What's not in this PR (memo'd)\n\nSee \\`docs/a11y-memo.md\\`:\n\n1. UserButton popover → migrate to \\`@base-ui/react/menu\\` (focus trap, Esc, role=menu, focus restore on close).\n2. Color contrast pass on \\`text-muted-foreground\\` against ivory background (likely ~3.5:1 on small body text, AA needs 4.5:1).\n3. Global \\`focus-visible\\` ring style — currently inconsistent across components.\n4. Wiring the now-placeholder Installed/Saved/Docs once the underlying routes exist.\n5. Mobile a11y not covered — needs a real-device session.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] \\`bun run validate\\` passes (192 → 193 tests; UserButton tests updated to assert aria-label / haspopup / expanded)\n- [ ] \\`/{en,zh}/extensions\\` renders with translated labels (no English in the zh page's filter / sidebar / topbar)\n- [ ] Tabbing from page load: skip link is the first focusable element; activating it lands focus in \\`<main>\\`\n- [ ] Tabbing past the sidebar: no longer stops at the Installed / Saved / collection rows\n- [ ] Filter chips / scope pills / tag chips: each toggle exposes \\`aria-pressed\\` reflecting active state\n- [ ] User-menu trigger: \\`aria-expanded\\` flips on click; \\`aria-haspopup=\"menu\"\\` present\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Open PR for a11y audit"
}

> AGENT

**Proposed commit:**

```
fix(a11y): aria-labels in user's current locale, not target locale

CodeRabbit caught that zh.json's switchToEn was left in English, but
the same inconsistency was in en.json (switchToZh was in Chinese).
The accessibility convention is: aria-label in the reader's current
language, not the target's — so a zh-locale screen-reader user hears
"切换到英文" rather than "Switch to English", and vice versa for en.

The visible button text already shows the target locale's name
("中文" / "EN") for visual recognition; the aria-label is purely
for AT and should match the user's reading language.
```

OK to commit and push?
