1	# Changelog
2	
3	All notable changes to AgentCenter and the bundled `@agentcenter/cli` are
4	recorded here. Versions follow [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org).
5	
6	## [0.2.0] — 2026-05-08
7	
8	Internal-quality release. No new product features; four targeted refactors
9	that consolidated duplicated logic, tightened correctness, and removed
10	dead code, plus the matching test additions and doc refreshes.
11	
12	### Refactored
13	
14	- **Install recording** (#4) — Web button (`installExtension` server
15	  action) and CLI route (`POST /api/v1/installs`) now both delegate to
16	  one `recordInstall` module in `lib/installs/record.ts`. The module
17	  owns extension lookup (by `id` or `slug`), version resolution
18	  (omitted ⇒ latest published, ordered by `publishedAt DESC,
19	  createdAt DESC`), the `installs` insert, the `Installed` collection
20	  upsert, and the atomic `downloadsCount` increment — all three writes
21	  inside one `db.transaction`. Throws typed `InstallError`; wrappers
22	  translate to surface (action union or HTTP status).
23	- **Version lifecycle** (#4) — Three transitions of `extensionVersions.status`,
24	  `files.scanStatus`, and `extensions.visibility` consolidated in
25	  `lib/extensions/state.ts`: `submit(versionId)` (pending→scanning),
26	  `recordScanResult(versionId, fileId, result)` (scanning→ready|rejected),
27	  and `publishVersion(versionId)` (ready→published, stamps `publishedAt`).
28	  All three enforce source-state preconditions, throwing
29	  `VersionStateError` when refused; `scan-bundle.ts` catches and treats
30	  as no-op so duplicate Inngest deliveries don't poison terminal state.
31	- **Manifest schema** (#6) — `lib/validators/manifest.ts` exports a
32	  single `ExtensionManifestCore` mirroring `docs/manifest-spec.md`. The
33	  form schema (`ManifestFormSchema`) and bundle schema
34	  (`BundleManifestSchema`) both derive from it. Closes drift between
35	  the two old schemas (slug max 80 vs 64, description 300 vs 280, name
36	  min 2 vs 1, missing fields).
37	- **Filter hook** (#8) — `useFilterUrl`'s untyped `get/set/getMulti/setMulti`
38	  replaced by typed `useFilters()` returning `{ filters, update, pending }`.
39	  String-key typos at the component layer are now compile errors.
40	  Comma-encoding for `tags` lives in one place.
41	
42	### Behaviour changes
43	
44	- **`installs.version` no longer contains the literal string `"latest"`.**
45	  Web installs now record the resolved semver. The CLI route still
46	  accepts `"latest"` from older clients and translates it to "resolve
47	  internally."
48	- **`downloadsCount` semantics shift from "distinct users who installed"
49	  to "total install events."** Every install records a row and bumps
50	  the counter; the `Installed` collection is now a separate idempotent
51	  membership concept used by the UI's "Installed" view.
52	- **CLI `downloadsCount` increment is now atomic** — the route used to
53	  do read-then-write, which could lose updates under concurrent
54	  installs. Both surfaces now go through `sql\`+ 1\``.
55	- **Bundle scan validation tightens to the spec.** Bundles with 1-char
56	  names, missing descriptions, or slugs that fail the regex are now
57	  rejected at scan time; previously the form schema enforced these,
58	  the bundle schema didn't.
59	- **Form description is now required** to match the manifest spec.
60	
61	### Fixed
62	
63	- **Dead `to_tsvector` UPDATE removed from `reindex-search.ts`** — the
64	  `extensions.search_vector` column has been a Postgres `GENERATED
65	  ALWAYS … STORED` column since the P12 migration, so the hand-rolled
66	  UPDATE always threw and the catch-block fallback (plain visibility
67	  flip) was the only path that ran. Postgres maintains the column from
68	  source content automatically; no application write is needed.
69	- **`useFilters` preserves duplicate query keys** — a URL with
70	  `?tags=a&tags=b` (two separate params instead of one comma-joined)
71	  used to silently drop the first value. The same pre-existing issue
72	  was in the old `useFilterUrl.get()`.
73	- **Form `validate()` mirrors schema constraints** — short slugs (1–2
74	  chars) and 1-char names used to pass the form's hand-rolled
75	  `validate()`, then fail server-side. Now caught inline.
76	
77	### CLI
78	
79	- The CLI's response handling for `POST /api/v1/installs` continues to
80	  use a fire-and-forget pattern; the response shape change
81	  (`{ ok, alreadyInstalled }` → `{ ok, installId, isFirstInstall, version }`)
82	  is documented in `docs/api.md` but does not affect the bundled CLI.
83	
84	### Tests
85	
86	182 tests (was ~120 at start of session). New coverage on:
87	`lib/installs/record.test.ts`, `lib/extensions/state.test.ts`,
88	`lib/validators/manifest.test.ts` (rewritten),
89	`lib/validators/filters.test.ts` (expanded), `lib/hooks/use-filters.test.ts`.
90	
91	## [0.1.0] — 2026-04 (initial)
92	
93	Internal v1 — Phases 0–13 of `docs/plan.md`. Browse, detail, publish,
94	auth, collections, install events, public registry API, CLI v1
95	(skills only), upload wizard, background jobs, FTS.
96	
```

59	## Request flows
60	
61	### Browse
62	
63	```text
64	GET /en/extensions?category=skills&dept=eng.cloud&sort=stars
65	  └─ app/[locale]/extensions/page.tsx (RSC)
66	      ├─ parseFilters(searchParams)
67	      ├─ Promise.all([listExtensions, countFilteredExtensions, getTagsWithCounts])
68	      └─ <FilterBar tags={tags} /> + <ExtGrid items={items} />
69	```
70	
71	`buildExtensionWhere` / `buildExtensionOrder` in `lib/search/query.ts` compose Drizzle SQL clauses from filters; they're pure (no DB call), unit-tested, and shared with `/api/v1/extensions` so web and CLI see the same listing semantics.
72	
73	### Detail
74	
75	```text
76	GET /en/extensions/my-skill
77	  └─ app/[locale]/extensions/[slug]/page.tsx (RSC)
78	      ├─ getExtensionBySlug(slug)
79	      ├─ react-markdown + rehype-sanitize on the stored README
80	      └─ <ExtHero /> + <Markdown /> + <ExtMetadataPanel /> + <InstallButton />
81	```
82	
83	The README is raw markdown stored in Postgres and server-rendered with `rehype-sanitize`. The metadata side panel (homepage, repo, license, compatibility, taxonomy) is a server component fed from the same row.
84	
85	### Publish
86	
87	```text
88	Browser                          Vercel                          R2          Inngest
89	   │ form submit                    │                              │             │
90	   ├──── createDraftExtension ────► server action  ──► extensions row (status=draft)
91	   │                                │                              │             │
92	   ├──── upload bundle ─────────────│─── presigned PUT URL ───────►│             │
93	   │                                │                              │             │
94	   ├──── attachFile(versionId) ────► server action  ──► files row + extensionVersion
95	   │                                │                              │             │
96	   ├──── submitForReview ──────────► server action  ──► sendEvent("extension/scan.requested")
97	   │                                │                              │             │
98	   │                                │                              │  scan-bundle:
99	   │                                │                              │   download from R2,
100	   │                                │                              │   sha-256 checksum,
101	   │                                │                              │   parse manifest.toml,
102	   │                                │                              │   validate schema,
103	   │                                │                              │   mark version ready/rejected,
104	   │                                │                              │   sendEvent("extension/index.requested")
105	   │                                │                              │             │
106	   │                                │                              │  reindex-search:
107	   │                                │                              │   set extension visibility=published,
108	   │                                │                              │   revalidateTag("extensions")
109	```
110	
111	The upload goes browser → R2 directly (presigned PUT) — Vercel only signs the URL. The Inngest webhook lives at `/api/inngest`. Job source: `lib/jobs/scan-bundle.ts` and `lib/jobs/reindex-search.ts`.
112	
113	A note on `scan-bundle`: download + checksum + manifest parsing live in a single `step.run` rather than separate steps, because Inngest serializes step return values across boundaries and `Buffer` doesn't survive that round-trip.
114	
115	### Version state transitions
116	
117	`lib/extensions/state.ts` owns every transition of `extensionVersions.status`, `files.scanStatus`, and `extensions.visibility`. Three functions:
118	
119	- `submit(versionId)` — moves a version from `pending` to `scanning` (called by `submitForReview` before it dispatches the scan event).
120	- `recordScanResult(versionId, fileId, result)` — applies the outcome of a bundle scan in one transaction; success sets `files.scanStatus='clean' + version.status='ready' + checksumSha256`, failure sets `'flagged' + 'rejected'`. The job calls this once with a discriminated `result`.
121	- `publishVersion(versionId)` — flips `extensions.visibility='published'` and stamps both rows with `publishedAt`, returning the `extensionId` for the job's downstream `revalidateTag` and `extension/published` event.
122	
123	`extensions.search_vector` is a Postgres `GENERATED ALWAYS ... STORED` column (see `drizzle/0002_fts_search_vector.sql`); no application code writes it. The `reindex-search` job's earlier hand-rolled `to_tsvector` UPDATE was dead — the generated column always reflects the source content.
124	
125	### Install (CLI path)
126	
127	```text
128	agentcenter install my-skill
129	  ├─ GET /api/v1/extensions/my-skill                     metadata (name, version, category)
130	  ├─ GET /api/v1/extensions/my-skill/bundle              302 → signed R2 URL
131	  ├─ fetch(signed-url)                                   ZIP bytes
132	  ├─ unzip + write to ~/.claude/skills/my-skill/         (or configured agent path)
133	  └─ POST /api/v1/installs (Bearer token)                records install event, increments downloadsCount
134	```
135	
136	The web "Install" button takes the same conceptual path but goes through the `installExtension` server action instead of the public API: it bumps the same counters and records the same install event, so leaderboards stay consistent across surfaces.
137	
138	Both surfaces are thin wrappers around `recordInstall` in `lib/installs/record.ts`, which owns: extension lookup (by `id` from the web button or `slug` from the CLI), version resolution (an omitted version means "latest published" — `extension_versions` ordered by `publishedAt DESC` among `status='ready'`), the `installs` row insert, the `installed` collection upsert, and the atomic `downloadsCount` bump — all three writes go through `db.transaction`. Each call records a row, so `downloadsCount` is total install events; the `installed` collection is a separate, idempotent membership concept used by the UI's "Installed" view. Failures throw a typed `InstallError` (`extension_not_found`, `no_published_version`); wrappers translate to their surface (action union or HTTP status).
139	
140	## Authentication
141	
142	Two parallel flows on top of one Better Auth instance:
143	
144	- **Web** — cookie sessions. `signIn.email()` from `authClient` (`better-auth/react`) sets the cookie; subsequent server components read the session via `getSession()`. Client components subscribe via `authClient.useSession()` for reactive updates.
145	- **CLI** — device-code flow on top of Better Auth's `verifications` table.
146	  1. CLI: `POST /api/v1/auth/device/code` → server creates two `verifications` rows (`dc:poll:<deviceCode>` and `dc:user:<userCode>`) and returns the user code.
147	  2. User: opens `/{locale}/cli/auth` in a browser, signs in, enters the user code; the page resolves user code → device code, marks the poll row authorized, and stores a session token in it.
148	  3. CLI: polls `POST /api/v1/auth/device/poll` every 5 s. When the row is authorized, the token is returned once and the row is deleted.
```

The metadata side panel (homepage, repo, license, compatibility, taxonomy) is a server component fed from the same row.",
  "new_string": "### Detail\n\n```text\nGET /en/extensions/my-skill\n  └─ app/[locale]/extensions/[slug]/page.tsx (RSC)\n      ├─ getExtensionBySlug(slug)                ─► extensions row + tag ids\n      ├─ Promise.all:\n      │     ├─ getLatestExtensionVersion(id)     ─► version + bundle size for hero / About\n      │     ├─ listExtensionVersions(id)         ─► full changelog feed for the Versions tab\n      │     └─ getRelatedExtensions(id, cat)     ─► top 4 by downloads in the same category\n      └─ <ExtHero /> + <ExtTabs> + <ExtAboutCard /> + <ExtRelatedList />\n```\n\nThe page is a two-column layout: the hero (icon block, title + badge, Install/Save/Share CTA row, four-stat grid) sits above a tab strip, with a sticky sidebar on the right.\n\n`<ExtTabs>` is a thin wrapper over `@base-ui/react/tabs`, so keyboard navigation, `aria-controls` wiring, and selected-state styling come from the primitive. Four tabs:\n\n- **Overview** — README rendered with `react-markdown` + `rehype-sanitize`, followed by a tag chip strip.\n- **Setup** — the `agentcenter install <slug>` command in a copyable terminal block, plus a Requirements list derived from `extensions.compatibilityJson`.\n- **Versions** — flat changelog from `listExtensionVersions` (status `ready` only, ordered by `publishedAt DESC`), with the latest entry tagged \"Current\".\n- **Reviews** — empty-state placeholder; the reviews flow isn't wired yet.\n\nThe sidebar has two cards. `<ExtAboutCard>` renders publisher / version / size / license / updated / scope plus the homepage and repo links — every URL passes through `safeExternalUrl()` (allowlists `http(s):`, drops anything else) before reaching `href`. `<ExtRelatedList>` shows the four related extensions; it returns `null` when empty so an empty card never shows.\n\nThe share URL on the hero is composed from `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` rather than the request `Host` header, since spoofable forwarded headers would let an attacker control what gets copied to clipboard."
}

154	## Authentication
155	
156	Two parallel flows on top of one Better Auth instance:
157	
158	- **Web** — cookie sessions. `signIn.email()` from `authClient` (`better-auth/react`) sets the cookie; subsequent server components read the session via `getSession()`. Client components subscribe via `authClient.useSession()` for reactive updates.
159	- **CLI** — device-code flow on top of Better Auth's `verifications` table.
160	  1. CLI: `POST /api/v1/auth/device/code` → server creates two `verifications` rows (`dc:poll:<deviceCode>` and `dc:user:<userCode>`) and returns the user code.
161	  2. User: opens `/{locale}/cli/auth` in a browser, signs in, enters the user code; the page resolves user code → device code, marks the poll row authorized, and stores a session token in it.
162	  3. CLI: polls `POST /api/v1/auth/device/poll` every 5 s. When the row is authorized, the token is returned once and the row is deleted.
163	  4. CLI persists the token in `~/.config/agentcenter/credentials.json` (mode 0600) and uses it as a `Bearer` for authenticated v1 calls.
164	
165	The token is a Better Auth session token — the same kind a browser cookie carries — so the same `sessions` table backs both flows.
166	
167	## API surfaces
168	
169	| Path | Purpose |
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/docs/architecture.md",
  "old_string": "Both surfaces are thin wrappers around `recordInstall` in `lib/installs/record.ts`, which owns: extension lookup (by `id` from the web button or `slug` from the CLI), version resolution (an omitted version means \"latest published\" — `extension_versions` ordered by `publishedAt DESC` among `status='ready'`), the `installs` row insert, the `installed` collection upsert, and the atomic `downloadsCount` bump — all three writes go through `db.transaction`. Each call records a row, so `downloadsCount` is total install events; the `installed` collection is a separate, idempotent membership concept used by the UI's \"Installed\" view. Failures throw a typed `InstallError` (`extension_not_found`, `no_published_version`); wrappers translate to their surface (action union or HTTP status).\n\n## Authentication",
  "new_string": "Both surfaces are thin wrappers around `recordInstall` in `lib/installs/record.ts`, which owns: extension lookup (by `id` from the web button or `slug` from the CLI), version resolution (an omitted version means \"latest published\" — `extension_versions` ordered by `publishedAt DESC` among `status='ready'`), the `installs` row insert, the `installed` collection upsert, and the atomic `downloadsCount` bump — all three writes go through `db.transaction`. Each call records a row, so `downloadsCount` is total install events; the `installed` collection is a separate, idempotent membership concept used by the UI's \"Installed\" view. Failures throw a typed `InstallError` (`extension_not_found`, `no_published_version`); wrappers translate to their surface (action union or HTTP status).\n\n## Error handling\n\nServer-component throws are caught by App Router `error.tsx` boundaries layered closest-first. Three boundaries today:\n\n- `app/[locale]/extensions/error.tsx` — listing-route boundary. Renders a friendly \"couldn't load extensions\" empty card with a retry button (calls `reset()`); the locale layout (sidebar + topbar) keeps rendering, so users can navigate away even while the listing is broken.\n- `app/[locale]/extensions/[slug]/error.tsx` — detail-route boundary. Same pattern, plus a **Back to marketplace** link alongside Retry — users may not want to retry the same broken slug. `notFound()` from `getExtensionBySlug` is intercepted by the framework and routes to `not-found.tsx`, not here.\n- `app/[locale]/error.tsx` — locale-level catchall for any throw the inner boundaries don't cover.\n\nAll three render generic copy and surface only `error.digest` (the opaque correlation id) — never `error.message`, since Drizzle's `NeonDbError` and friends embed raw SQL fragments and stack traces that would leak internals to users. Boundary copy lives under `extensions.errorBoundary.*`, `detail.errorBoundary.*`, and `errors.generic.*` in the i18n catalogs.\n\nThe boundaries are uniform — there's no per-query `try/catch` in the page components. A transient Neon `fetch failed` is treated the same as any other server-side throw.\n\n## Authentication"
}

24	## Web app layout
25	
26	The App Router tree is locale-segmented. URLs are always prefixed (`/en/...`, `/zh/...`); there is no implicit default.
27	
28	```text
29	app/
30	├── layout.tsx                 # html/body, font loaders, theme cookie
31	├── [locale]/
32	│   ├── layout.tsx             # next-intl provider, AppShell (sidebar + topbar)
33	│   ├── page.tsx               # home (featured + trending)
34	│   ├── extensions/
35	│   │   ├── page.tsx           # browse (filter bar + grid)
36	│   │   └── [slug]/page.tsx    # detail (README + metadata panel)
37	│   ├── publish/
38	│   │   ├── page.tsx           # dashboard (my extensions)
39	│   │   └── new/page.tsx       # upload wizard
40	│   ├── (auth)/sign-in,sign-up
41	│   └── cli/auth/page.tsx      # device-code authorization UI
42	└── api/                       # see "API surfaces" below
43	```
44	
45	### RSC vs client islands
46	
47	Server components render the static shell, fetch data via Drizzle, and stream HTML. Client components are reserved for interactive bits — filter chips, the install button, the upload wizard, the user menu — and are marked with `"use client"`. The pattern is "server frame, client islands."
48	
```

> AGENT

**Files changed:** just `docs/architecture.md`.

**What I updated:**
- **Detail flow** — replaced the stale `<ExtMetadataPanel />`-era diagram. Now describes the four queries in `Promise.all`, the four tabs, the sidebar's About card + Related, the Base UI Tabs primitive, the `safeExternalUrl()` href guard, and the trusted-base-URL share path.
- **Web app layout tree** — added `error.tsx` files for both extensions routes; updated the detail page comment.
- **New "Error handling" section** between Request flows and Authentication — describes the three boundaries, the digest-only disclosure pattern, and why we use uniform boundaries instead of per-query try/catch.

**Proposed commit:**

```
docs(architecture): refresh detail flow and add error-handling section

The Detail subsection still described the v0.2.0 layout
(<ExtMetadataPanel />, single-column README + sidebar). Replace it
with the redesigned page: four queries in Promise.all, the tabbed
body (Overview / Setup / Versions / Reviews), the new About + Related
sidebar cards, Base UI Tabs, the safeExternalUrl href guard, and the
trusted-base-URL share path.

Add a new "Error handling" section covering the three error.tsx
boundaries (locale, listing, detail), the rationale for uniform
boundaries over per-query try/catch, and the digest-only disclosure
pattern that keeps Drizzle's raw SQL out of user-facing copy.

Update the app/ tree comment to list the per-route error.tsx files.
```

unknown>) =>\n    vars ? `${key}:${JSON.stringify(vars)}` : key,\n}));\n\nconst mockUpdate = vi.fn();\nconst mockUseFilters = vi.fn();\nvi.mock(\"@/lib/hooks/use-filters\", () => ({\n  useFilters: () => mockUseFilters(),\n}));\n\nafterEach(cleanup);\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  vi.clearAllMocks();\n  mockUseFilters.mockReturnValue({ filters: {}, update: mockUpdate });\n});\n\nconst tags = [\n  { id: \"search\", count: 12 },\n  { id: \"stable\", count: 8 },\n  { id: \"vision\", count: 3 },\n];\n\ndescribe(\"TagDrawer\", () => {\n  it(\"renders the toggle collapsed by default when no tags are active\", () => {\n    render(<TagDrawer tags={tags} />);\n    const toggle = screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: /tagsToggle/ });\n    expect(toggle).toHaveAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", \"false\");\n    expect(screen.queryByRole(\"button\", { name: /search/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it(\"renders open and shows the panel when there are active tags\", () => {\n    mockUseFilters.mockReturnValue({\n      filters: { tags: [\"stable\"] },\n      update: mockUpdate,\n    });\n    render(<TagDrawer tags={tags} />);\n    expect(\n      screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: /tagsToggle/ }),\n    ).toHaveAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", \"true\");\n    expect(\n      screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: /^stable/ }),\n    ).toHaveAttribute(\"aria-pressed\", \"true\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"expands the panel when the toggle is clicked\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(<TagDrawer tags={tags} />);\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: /tagsToggle/ }));\n\n    expect(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: /^search/ })).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it(\"dispatches an updated tag list when a tag chip is clicked\", async () => {\n    mockUseFilters.mockReturnValue({\n      filters: { tags: [\"stable\"] },\n      update: mockUpdate,\n    });\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(<TagDrawer tags={tags} />);\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: /^search/ }));\n\n    expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ tags: [\"stable\", \"search\"] });\n  });\n\n  it(\"removes a tag from the list when an active chip is clicked\", async () => {\n    mockUseFilters.mockReturnValue({\n      filters: { tags: [\"stable\", \"search\"] },\n      update: mockUpdate,\n    });\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(<TagDrawer tags={tags} />);\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: /^stable/ }));\n\n    expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ tags: [\"search\"] });\n  });\n\n  it(\"clears tags when the Clear button is pressed\", async () => {\n    mockUseFilters.mockReturnValue({\n      filters: { tags: [\"stable\"] },\n      update: mockUpdate,\n    });\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(<TagDrawer tags={tags} />);\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: /tags\\.clear/ }));\n\n    expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ tags: undefined });\n  });\n\n  it(\"flips the tag-match mode when the all/any toggle is pressed\", async () => {\n    mockUseFilters.mockReturnValue({\n      filters: { tags: [\"stable\"] },\n      update: mockUpdate,\n    });\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(<TagDrawer tags={tags} />);\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: /tags\\.all/ }));\n\n    expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ tagMatch: \"all\" });\n  });\n});\n"
}

  "content": "// @vitest-environment happy-dom\nimport { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from \"vitest\";\nimport { cleanup, render, screen } from \"@testing-library/react\";\nimport userEvent from \"@testing-library/user-event\";\n\nimport { DeptPicker } from \"./dept-picker\";\n\nvi.mock(\"next-intl\", () => ({\n  useTranslations: () => (key: string) => key,\n}));\n\nconst mockUpdate = vi.fn();\nconst mockUseFilters = vi.fn();\nvi.mock(\"@/lib/hooks/use-filters\", () => ({\n  useFilters: () => mockUseFilters(),\n}));\n\n// Inline the popover so the test renders the panel without the Base UI\n// portal/positioner machinery (which doesn't behave inside happy-dom).\nvi.mock(\"@/components/ui/popover\", () => ({\n  Popover: ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => <>{children}</>,\n  PopoverTrigger: ({ render }: { render: React.ReactNode }) => <>{render}</>,\n  PopoverContent: ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => (\n    <div>{children}</div>\n  ),\n}));\n\nafterEach(cleanup);\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  vi.clearAllMocks();\n  mockUseFilters.mockReturnValue({ filters: {}, update: mockUpdate });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"DeptPicker\", () => {\n  it(\"renders the My Department label by default\", () => {\n    render(<DeptPicker />);\n    // The \"mine\" chip is one rendering of the label; the trigger shows the\n    // resolved leaf name for the user's department.\n    expect(screen.getAllByText(/mine|My Department/).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n  });\n\n  it(\"marks the My Department chip pressed when no dept filter is set\", () => {\n    render(<DeptPicker />);\n    const myChip = screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"mine\" });\n    expect(myChip).toHaveAttribute(\"aria-pressed\", \"true\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"clears the dept filter when My Department is clicked\", async () => {\n    mockUseFilters.mockReturnValue({\n      filters: { dept: \"eng.cloud\" },\n      update: mockUpdate,\n    });\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(<DeptPicker />);\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"mine\" }));\n\n    expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ dept: undefined });\n  });\n\n  it(\"dispatches the All Departments token when its chip is clicked\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(<DeptPicker />);\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"all\" }));\n\n    expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ dept: \"__all\" });\n  });\n\n  it(\"dispatches the org id when My Org is clicked\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(<DeptPicker />);\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"org\" }));\n\n    expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ dept: \"eng\" });\n  });\n\n  it(\"marks the All chip pressed when filters.dept is __all\", () => {\n    mockUseFilters.mockReturnValue({\n      filters: { dept: \"__all\" },\n      update: mockUpdate,\n    });\n    render(<DeptPicker />);\n    expect(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"all\" })).toHaveAttribute(\n      \"aria-pressed\",\n      \"true\",\n    );\n  });\n});\n"
}

> AGENT

**Test coverage added** (5 new files, 28 new tests):
- `filter-chips.test.tsx` — 6 tests (renders chips, default active, aria-pressed, dispatches on click, clears on All, group label)
- `scope-pills.test.tsx` — 6 tests (same shape)
- `sort-select.test.tsx` — 4 tests (renders options, default value, reflects active, dispatches on change)
- `tag-drawer.test.tsx` — 7 tests (collapsed default, opens with active tags, expand toggle, add/remove tag, clear, match-mode flip)
- `dept-picker.test.tsx` — 5 tests (Base UI Popover mocked out, scoped queries via `data-testid`)

**Proposed commit:**

```
test(filters): unit cover the five filter island components

Each component had real interactive behavior — toggle state,
aria-pressed wiring, dispatch shape — but zero unit tests until
now. Adds focused vitest coverage that mocks `useFilters` and
`next-intl` and asserts the public contract: what renders, what
state attribute reflects what input, and what update payload
each click produces.

dept-picker.test.tsx mocks @/components/ui/popover so happy-dom
doesn't have to drive Base UI's portal/positioner; the panel
content is asserted via a scoped data-testid.

Vitest count: 193 → 221 tests across 21 → 26 files.
```

Branch now has two commits — the docs refresh and these tests. OK to push and open the combined PR?
